// Filtered excerpt: some source lines are omitted.
import { execFile } from "node:child_process";
import fs from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import { promisify } from "node:util";
import { SESSION_INSTRUCTIONS } from "./console-mcp.mjs";

// The shared "brain" wiki (~/repos/brain, see its CLAUDE.md) is one memory for every agent on this machine. Claude Code reaches it through
// ...
//   recall: hand the harness only the same brain index the Claude hook injects when its process starts
//   save:   agents edit wiki pages themselves; after each turn we run `brain commit` (file-locked, idempotent)
export const BRAIN_BIN =
  process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_BRAIN || path.join(os.homedir(), "repos", "brain", "bin", "brain");
// `brain context` prints wiki/index.md. Run it off the event loop (a sync exec stalled every request ~250 ms) and keep the result until
// ...
const TTL = 5 * 60_000;
const INDEX =
  process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_BRAIN_INDEX ||
  path.join(path.dirname(path.dirname(BRAIN_BIN)), "wiki", "index.md");
const run = promisify(execFile);
let cache: { at: number; stamp: string; text: string } | undefined;
let inflight: { stamp: string; promise: Promise<string> } | undefined;
const indexStamp = () => {
  try {
    const s = fs.statSync(INDEX);
    return `${s.mtimeMs}:${s.size}`;
  } catch {
    return "";
  }
};

export const brainAvailable = () => fs.existsSync(BRAIN_BIN);

export function brainContext(): Promise<string> {
  if (!brainAvailable()) return Promise.resolve("");
  const stamp = indexStamp();
  if (cache && cache.stamp === stamp && Date.now() - cache.at < TTL)
    return Promise.resolve(cache.text);
  if (inflight?.stamp === stamp) return inflight.promise;
  const promise: Promise<string> = run(BRAIN_BIN, ["context"], {
    encoding: "utf8",
    timeout: 5000,
    env: { ...process.env, BRAIN_NO_NUDGE: "1" },
  })
    .then(
      (r) => String(r.stdout).trim(),
      () => "",
    )
    .then((text) => {
      cache = { at: Date.now(), stamp, text };
      if (inflight?.promise === promise) inflight = undefined;
      return text;
    });
  inflight = { stamp, promise };
  return promise;
}

export function brainCommit() {
  if (brainAvailable()) execFile(BRAIN_BIN, ["commit"], { timeout: 15_000 }, () => {});
}

// Reply-style preamble (ADHD + caveman ultra) shared by every harness, replacing the always-loaded caveman / i-have-adhd skills.
export const STYLE_FILE =
  process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_STYLE ||
  path.join(os.homedir(), "repos", "brain", "config", "style-preamble.md");

export function stylePreamble(): string {
  try {
    return fs.readFileSync(STYLE_FILE, "utf8").trim();
  } catch {
    return "";
  }
}

// ...
export const personaPath = (name: string) =>
  path.join(
    process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_PERSONA_DIR || path.join(os.homedir(), ".claude", "agents"),
    `${name}.md`,
  );

/** The persona for a main thread: reviewer for an Auto review run, dream for the nightly dream, else orchestrator. */
export function personaFor(spec: {
  issueRun?: { role: string };
  schedule?: { task: string };
}): string {
  if (spec.issueRun?.role === "review") return "reviewer";
  if (spec.schedule?.task === "dream") return "dream";
  return "orchestrator";
}

const personaCache = new Map<string, { stamp: string; body: string }>();

/** The persona body without its frontmatter, cached on mtimeMs:size; empty when the file is missing. */
export function personaInstructions(name: string): string {
  const file = personaPath(name);
  try {
    const stat = fs.statSync(file);
    const stamp = `${stat.mtimeMs}:${stat.size}`;
    const hit = personaCache.get(file);
    if (hit?.stamp === stamp) return hit.body;
    const body = fs
      .readFileSync(file, "utf8")
      .replace(/^---\r?\n[\s\S]*?\r?\n---\r?\n?/, "")
      .trim();
    personaCache.set(file, { stamp, body });
    return body;
  } catch {
    return "";
  }
}

/** Keep the write contract discoverable without loading the schema or full memory pages. */
export function brainInstructions(): string {
  if (!brainAvailable()) return "";
  const root = path.dirname(path.dirname(BRAIN_BIN));
  return [
    "# Shared brain: required save workflow",
    `Use the shared memory repository at ${root}; do not create another memory store.`,
    `Before any memory write, read ${path.join(root, "CLAUDE.md")} and follow its schema.`,
    `Use the full CLI path ${BRAIN_BIN}; do not assume brain is on PATH.`,
    "Search existing pages first. Save only durable preferences, decisions, and non-obvious lessons; exclude code inventories, test status, and conversation dumps.",
    "Update flat wiki/<lowercase-kebab-slug>.md pages with required frontmatter and concise facts. Add one lean line `- wiki/<slug>: summary` to its topic file wiki/index-<topic>.md; top-level wiki/index.md lists topics only.",
    `Procedures and operating notes go in the ops/ layer. Tag every page with 1-8 atomic tags: run ${BRAIN_BIN} tags suggest for candidates, reuse existing tags before inventing one, and keep each tag a single concept.`,
    `Append the log using ${BRAIN_BIN} log, run ${BRAIN_BIN} lint and fix findings, then run ${BRAIN_BIN}-doctor and apply its fixes before claiming the save is complete.`,
// ...
    "Automatic recall below contains only the top-level index. Open topic files and pages on demand; brain search finds the rest.",
  ].join("\n");
}

/** Style, memory write contract, and index for harnesses accepting one instruction string. */
export const harnessInstructions = async () =>
  [stylePreamble(), brainInstructions(), SESSION_INSTRUCTIONS, await brainContext()]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join("\n\n");

// Filtered excerpt: some source lines are omitted.
import fs from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";

export type ClaudeAccount = "primary" | "secondary";

export function normalizeClaudeAccount(value?: string): ClaudeAccount {
  if (value === undefined || value === "primary") return "primary";
  if (value === "secondary") return "secondary";
  throw new Error("Unknown Claude account.");
}

export function claudeConfigDir(account?: string, env = process.env, home = os.homedir()): string {
  return normalizeClaudeAccount(account) === "secondary"
    ? path.join(home, ".claude-secondary")
    : env.CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR || path.join(home, ".claude");
}

export function claudeAccountReady(
  account?: string,
  env = process.env,
  home = os.homedir(),
): boolean {
  try {
    const selected = normalizeClaudeAccount(account);
    const config = claudeConfigDir(selected, env, home);
    const oauth = JSON.parse(
      fs.readFileSync(path.join(config, ".credentials.json"), "utf8"),
    ).claudeAiOauth;
    if (typeof oauth?.accessToken !== "string" || !oauth.accessToken.length) return false;
    const metadata = path.join(config, ".claude.json");
    const legacy =
      selected === "primary" && !env.CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR ? path.join(home, ".claude.json") : undefined;
    const file = fs.existsSync(metadata) ? metadata : legacy;
    const email =
      file && fs.existsSync(file)
        ? JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8")).oauthAccount?.emailAddress
        : undefined;
    const expected = selected === "secondary" ? "REDACTED_EMAIL" : "REDACTED_EMAIL";
    if (selected === "secondary" && typeof email !== "string") return false;
    if (typeof email === "string" && email.toLowerCase() !== expected) return false;
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export function requireClaudeAccount(account?: string): ClaudeAccount {
  const selected = normalizeClaudeAccount(account);
  if (selected === "secondary" && !claudeAccountReady(selected))
    throw new Error(
      "secondary Claude account is not signed in. Complete its separate Claude login first.",
    );
  return selected;
}

// ...
  normalizeClaudeAccount(account) === "secondary" ? "claude:secondary" : "claude";

export function claudeAccounts() {
  return [
    {
      id: "primary",
      label: "primary",
      email: "REDACTED_EMAIL",
      ready: claudeAccountReady("primary"),
    },
    {
      id: "secondary",
      label: "secondary",
      email: "REDACTED_EMAIL",
      ready: claudeAccountReady("secondary"),
    },
  ];
}

import os from "node:os";

// never let the subscription login be silently replaced by metered API billing
const SCRUBBED_ENV = [
  "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY",
  "ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN",
  "CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN",
  "CLAUDECODE",
];

// ...
// connectors plus project servers). Threads load only the servers named in AGENT_CONSOLE_MCP (comma list from ~/.claude.json, default none:
// agents use the codegraph CLI, so no per-thread MCP server stays alive); AGENT_CONSOLE_MCP=all restores everything.
export function leanMcpArgs(): string[] {
  const keep = (process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_MCP ?? "")
    .split(",")
    .map((x) => x.trim())
    .filter(Boolean);
  const consoleServer = consoleMcpServer();
  let servers: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  try {
    const all =
      JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(os.homedir(), ".claude.json"), "utf8")).mcpServers ?? {};
    servers = keep.includes("all")
      ? { ...all }
      : Object.fromEntries(keep.filter((k) => all[k]).map((k) => [k, all[k]]));
  } catch {}
  if (consoleServer) servers.agent_console = consoleServer;
  // always strict: a thread's tools array must not depend on account-level or project-level MCP config
  return ["--strict-mcp-config", "--mcp-config", JSON.stringify({ mcpServers: servers })];
}

export function normalizeCommands(raw: any[]): Command[] {
  return (raw ?? [])
    .map((c) => (typeof c === "string" ? { name: c } : c))
    .filter((c) => typeof c?.name === "string")
    .map((c) => ({
      name: c.name.replace(/^\/+/, ""),
      description: String(c.description ?? "").slice(0, 200),
      argumentHint: c.argumentHint || undefined,
    }))
    .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
}

// The SDK control protocol (`--permission-prompt-tool stdio`): the CLI asks its host before any tool that needs the user, as
//   {"type":"control_request","request_id":R,"request":{"subtype":"can_use_tool","tool_name":T,"input":{...},"tool_use_id":U}}
// and blocks until {"type":"control_response","response":{"subtype":"success","request_id":R,"response":{"behavior":"allow"|"deny",...}}}.
// Without a host, headless Claude drops AskUserQuestion from its tools altogether. AskUserQuestion becomes a question card; its
// answer goes back as updatedInput.answers {question text: "label, label"} (verified on CLI 2.1.301: the tool result reads
// `"Which color?"="Green"`). Every other prompt is denied, exactly as without a host, so the permission mode still decides.
interface PendingControl {
  input: any;
  questions: ReturnType<typeof cleanQuestions>;
}

interface ControlContext {
  pending: Map<string, PendingControl>;
  write: (msg: object) => void;
  emit: (event: any) => void;
  mode: string;
}

const AUTO_ALLOWED_TOOLS = [
  "mcp__agent_console__session_search",
  "mcp__agent_console__session_read",
// ...
  "mcp__agent_console__cli_search",
  "mcp__agent_console__thread_list",
  "mcp__agent_console__thread_send",
];

function respondControl(ctx: ControlContext, requestId: string, response: object) {
  ctx.write({
    type: "control_response",
    response: { subtype: "success", request_id: requestId, response },
  });
}

function askControlQuestions(ctx: ControlContext, event: any, req: any) {
  const questions = cleanQuestions(
    req.input.questions.map((question: any, idx: number) => ({
      ...question,
      id: String(idx),
      allowOther: true,
    })),
  );
  if (!questions.length) {
    respondControl(ctx, event.request_id, {
      behavior: "deny",
      message: "No questions were provided. Continue with your best judgment.",
    });
    return;
  }
  ctx.pending.set(event.request_id, { input: req.input, questions });
  ctx.emit({
    type: "question",
    id: event.request_id,
    tool_use_id: req.tool_use_id,
    questions,
    ts: Date.now(),
  });
}

function handleControlRequest(ctx: ControlContext, event: any) {
  const req = event.request ?? {};
  if (req.subtype !== "can_use_tool") {
    ctx.write({
      type: "control_response",
      response: {
        subtype: "error",
        request_id: event.request_id,
        error: `Unsupported control request: ${req.subtype}`,
      },
    });
    return;
  }
  // Console tools are authenticated by the bridge; thread sends also require a live root tool call.
  // Allow only these exact names in Plan/acceptEdits; all other permission prompts retain their policy.
// ...
    respondControl(ctx, event.request_id, { behavior: "allow", updatedInput: req.input ?? {} });
    return;
  }
  if (req.tool_name === "AskUserQuestion" && Array.isArray(req.input?.questions)) {
    askControlQuestions(ctx, event, req);
    return;
  }
  respondControl(ctx, event.request_id, {
    behavior: "deny",
    message: `Permission for ${req.tool_name} was not granted: this console does not approve tool prompts in ${ctx.mode} mode. Continue without it or ask the user to switch the thread to Bypass.`,
  });
}

function handleControlMessage(ctx: ControlContext, event: any): boolean {
  if (event?.type === "control_cancel_request") {
    if (ctx.pending.delete(event.request_id))
      ctx.emit({ type: "question_answer", id: event.request_id, cancelled: true });
    return true;
  }
  if (event?.type === "control_response") return true; // replies to host requests; we send none
  if (event?.type !== "control_request") return false;
  handleControlRequest(ctx, event);
  return true;
}

function answerControl(ctx: ControlContext, questionId: string, reply: QuestionReply): boolean {
  const entry = ctx.pending.get(questionId);
  if (!entry) throw new Error("This question is no longer waiting for an answer.");
  if (reply.skip || reply.note !== undefined) {
    const message =
      reply.note !== undefined
        ? `The user did not pick an option and replied in chat instead: ${reply.note}`
        : "The user skipped these questions without answering. Continue with your best judgment and do not ask the same questions again.";
    respondControl(ctx, questionId, { behavior: "deny", message });
    ctx.pending.delete(questionId);
    return reply.note !== undefined;
  }
  const answers: Record<string, string> = {};
  entry.questions.forEach((question, idx) => {
    const picked = (reply.answers[question.id] ?? [])
      .map((part) => String(part).trim())
      .filter(Boolean);
    if (picked.length)
      answers[String(entry.input.questions[idx]?.question ?? question.question)] =
        picked.join(", ");
  });
  respondControl(ctx, questionId, { behavior: "allow", updatedInput: { ...entry.input, answers } });
  ctx.pending.delete(questionId);
  return false;
}

// The SDK control protocol (`--permission-prompt-tool stdio`): the CLI asks its host before any tool that needs the user, as
//   {"type":"control_request","request_id":R,"request":{"subtype":"can_use_tool","tool_name":T,"input":{...},"tool_use_id":U}}
// and blocks until {"type":"control_response","response":{"subtype":"success","request_id":R,"response":{"behavior":"allow"|"deny",...}}}.
// Without a host, headless Claude drops AskUserQuestion from its tools altogether. AskUserQuestion becomes a question card; its
// answer goes back as updatedInput.answers {question text: "label, label"} (verified on CLI 2.1.301: the tool result reads
// `"Which color?"="Green"`). Every other prompt is denied, exactly as without a host, so the permission mode still decides.
export function claudeControl(
  write: (msg: object) => void,
  emit: (event: any) => void,
  mode: string,
) {
  const ctx: ControlContext = { pending: new Map(), write, emit, mode };
  return {
    pending: ctx.pending,
    cancelAll() {
      for (const questionId of ctx.pending.keys())
        emit({ type: "question_answer", id: questionId, cancelled: true });
      ctx.pending.clear();
    },
    // true when the line was a control message (never shown or logged)
    handle(event: any): boolean {
      return handleControlMessage(ctx, event);
    },
    answer(questionId: string, reply: QuestionReply): boolean {
      return answerControl(ctx, questionId, reply);
    },
  };
}

// Empty string disables the advisor in the installed CLI settings schema; omitted inherits user/project settings.
export function claudeAdvisorArgs(advisor?: string): string[] {
  if (advisor === "off") return ["--settings", JSON.stringify({ advisorModel: "" })];
  return advisor && advisor !== "default" ? ["--advisor", advisor] : [];
}

// A nested console can inherit its parent's local proxy URL. Never keep that
// managed URL when this console has disabled the proxy or cannot reach it.
export function claudeRoutingEnv(env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv): NodeJS.ProcessEnv {
  const routed = { ...env };
// ...
    delete routed.ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL;
// ...
  if (proxy && !routed.ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL) routed.ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL = proxy;
  return routed;
}

interface ClaudeRun {
  proc: ReturnType<typeof spawnLines> | undefined;
  requests: number;
}

export function claudeStartArgs(thread: ThreadSpec): string[] {
  const args = [
    "-p",
    "--input-format",
    "stream-json",
    "--output-format",
    "stream-json",
    "--verbose",
    "--include-partial-messages",
    "--permission-mode",
    thread.mode,
    "--permission-prompt-tool",
    "stdio", // host answers prompts over stdin/stdout: enables AskUserQuestion (see claudeControl)
    "--add-dir",
    threadDir(thread.id), // attachments live outside the project; without this plan/accept-edits could not read them
    "--model",
    thread.model,
  ];
  if (thread.effort && thread.effort !== "default") args.push("--effort", thread.effort);
  args.push(...claudeConsoleSettingsArgs(thread.advisor));
  args.push(...leanMcpArgs());
  const persona = personaFor(thread);
  if (fs.existsSync(personaPath(persona))) args.push("--agent", persona); // replaces the default system prompt; the persona carries the role rules
  const style = stylePreamble();
  if (style) args.push("--append-system-prompt", style); // brain index arrives via the user hook
  fs.mkdirSync(threadDir(thread.id), { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
  if (thread.sessionId) args.push("--resume", thread.sessionId);
  else if (thread.forkFrom) args.push("--resume", thread.forkFrom, "--fork-session"); // a forked thread: continue a copy of the parent session
  return args;
}

function claudeStartEnv(thread: ThreadSpec, account: ReturnType<typeof requireClaudeAccount>) {
  const env = claudeRoutingEnv(process.env);
  env.AGENT_CONSOLE_THREAD = thread.id;
  env.ENABLE_TOOL_SEARCH = "false"; // deferred tool loading changes the tools array mid-thread and busts the prompt cache
  const toolEnv = toolEnvFile();
  if (toolEnv && !env.CLAUDE_ENV_FILE) env.CLAUDE_ENV_FILE = toolEnv; // Bash commands start at the lower tool priority
  if (account === "secondary") env.CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR = claudeConfigDir(account);
  for (const name of SCRUBBED_ENV) delete env[name];
  return env;
}

function clipToolResults(event: any) {
  delete event.tool_use_result; // duplicate of message.content, can be huge
  for (const block of event.message?.content ?? []) {
    if (block?.type === "tool_result" && typeof block.content === "string")
      block.content = clip(block.content);
  }
}

function handleClaudeLine(
  thread: ThreadSpec,
  ctx: AgentCtx,
  control: ReturnType<typeof claudeControl>,
  line: string,
) {
  const event = parseJson(line);
  if (!event) return;
  if (control.handle(event)) return;
  if (event.type === "result") control.cancelAll();
  if (event.type === "user") clipToolResults(event);
  if (event.type === "system" && event.subtype === "init" && Array.isArray(event.slash_commands))
    ctx.commands(normalizeCommands(event.slash_commands));
  if (event.type === "system" && event.subtype === "commands_changed")
    return ctx.commands(normalizeCommands(event.commands));
// ...
  ctx.event(event);
}

// plain string when there is nothing to attach; otherwise the Messages API block form
function writeClaudeUser(proc: NonNullable<ClaudeRun["proc"]>, text: string, images: Attachment[]) {
  const content = images.length
    ? [
        ...images.map((image) => ({
          type: "image",
          source: {
            type: "base64",
            media_type: image.media_type,
            data: fs.readFileSync(image.path).toString("base64"),
          },
        })),
        ...(text.trim() ? [{ type: "text", text }] : []),
      ]
    : text;
  proc.write(JSON.stringify({ type: "user", message: { role: "user", content } }));
}

function claudeEffortLevel(effort?: string) {
  return !!effort && effort !== "default" && effort !== "ultracode"; // default/ultracode change flags, not a level
}

function configureClaude(run: ClaudeRun, from: Settings, target: Settings): boolean {
  // the CLI refuses to enter bypassPermissions unless launched in it ("bypass_not_launched"): restart for that
  if (target.mode === "bypassPermissions" && from.mode !== target.mode) return false;
  if (
    from.advisor !== target.advisor ||
    (from.effort !== target.effort &&
      !(claudeEffortLevel(from.effort) && claudeEffortLevel(target.effort)))
  )
    return false;
  const ask = (request: object) =>
    run.proc!.write(
      JSON.stringify({
        type: "control_request",
        request_id: `cfg-${++run.requests}`,
        request,
      }),
    );
  if (from.model !== target.model) ask({ subtype: "set_model", model: target.model });
  if (from.mode !== target.mode) ask({ subtype: "set_permission_mode", mode: target.mode });
  if (from.effort !== target.effort)
    ask({ subtype: "apply_flag_settings", settings: { effortLevel: target.effort } });
  return true;
}

export const claude: Provider = {
  id: "claude",
  label: "Claude Code",
  modes: ["plan", "acceptEdits", "bypassPermissions"],
  defaultMode: "acceptEdits",
  advisors: ["off", "opus", "fable"],
  steer: true,
  models: () => ["sonnet", "opus", "haiku"],
  defaultModel: () => "sonnet",
  modelDetails: () =>
    Object.fromEntries(
      ["sonnet", "opus", "haiku"].map((id) => [
        id,
        {
          label: id[0].toUpperCase() + id.slice(1),
          efforts: id === "haiku" ? [] : ["low", "medium", "high", "xhigh", "max", "ultracode"],
        },
      ]),
    ),
  status: () => ({ ok: true }),
  start(t: ThreadSpec, ctx: AgentCtx): Agent {
    const account = requireClaudeAccount(t.claudeAccount);
    const args = claudeStartArgs(t);
    const env = claudeStartEnv(t, account);
    const run: ClaudeRun = { proc: undefined, requests: 0 };
    const control = claudeControl((msg) => run.proc!.write(JSON.stringify(msg)), ctx.event, t.mode);
    const launch = sandboxLaunch(t, "claude", args, env);
    const proc = spawnLines(
      "claude",
      launch.command,
      launch.args,
      { cwd: t.cwd, env: launch.env },
      {
        ...ctx,
        exited(info) {
          control.cancelAll();
          ctx.exited(info);
        },
      },
      (line) => handleClaudeLine(t, ctx, control, line),
    );
    run.proc = proc;
    return {
      get alive() {
        return proc.alive();
      },
      send: (text, images = []) => writeClaudeUser(proc, text, images),
      compact: (instructions = "") =>
        writeClaudeUser(proc, `/compact${instructions ? " " + instructions : ""}`, []),
      // Claude takes a user message mid-turn on stdin and folds it in after the running tool call (verified: it dropped the
      // remaining commands and answered the new message within the same turn).
      steer: (text, images = []) => writeClaudeUser(proc, text, images),
      answer: (id, reply) => control.answer(id, reply),
// ...
      // set_model, set_permission_mode (except into bypass) and apply_flag_settings {effortLevel} change the next request, no model call.
      interrupt: () => {
        control.cancelAll();
        proc.write(
          JSON.stringify({
            type: "control_request",
            request_id: `int-${++run.requests}`,
            request: { subtype: "interrupt" },
          }),
        );
      },
      configure: (from, target) => configureClaude(run, from, target),
      abort: () => {
        control.cancelAll();
        proc.abort();
      },
      kill: () => {
        control.cancelAll();
        proc.kill();
      },
    };
  },
};

const ITEM_TYPES: Record<string, string> = {
  agentMessage: "agent_message",
  commandExecution: "command_execution",
  fileChange: "file_change",
  mcpToolCall: "mcp_tool_call",
  webSearch: "web_search",
};

// App-server thread item -> the exec-style row codexTranslator understands; undefined when the item has no visible row.
export function adaptItem(item: any, done: boolean): any {
  if (item.type === "reasoning")
    return done
      ? {
          type: "item.completed",
          item: { ...item, text: [...(item.summary || []), ...(item.content || [])].join("\n") },
        }
      : undefined;
  if (!ITEM_TYPES[item.type]) return undefined;
  return {
    type: done ? "item.completed" : "item.started",
    item: {
      ...item,
      type: ITEM_TYPES[item.type],
      aggregated_output: item.aggregatedOutput,
      exit_code: item.exitCode,
      ...(item.changes
        ? {
            changes: item.changes.map((change: any) => ({
              ...change,
              kind: change.kind?.type || change.kind,
            })),
          }
        : {}),
    },
  };
}


================
File: server/providers/codex-app-questions.ts
================
import { cleanQuestions, type Question, type QuestionReply } from "./types.ts";

export interface CodexQuestionCard {
  questions: Question[];
  consumesNote: boolean;
  reply(answer: QuestionReply): any;
}

const FORM_FIELD_TYPES = ["string", "boolean", "number", "integer", "array"];

// Codex asks the user through app-server server requests (exec mode refuses: "request_user_input not supported in exec mode"):
//   item/tool/requestUserInput   {questions:[{id,header,question,isOther,isSecret,options:[{label,description}]|null}]}
//                                -> {answers:{[id]:{answers:[...]}}}; no answers = "continue with best judgment"
//   mcpServer/elicitation/request (mode "form") an MCP server's JSON-schema form -> {action:"accept"|"decline", content}
// request_user_input is offered to the model in Plan collaboration mode, or in default mode behind the
// default_mode_request_user_input feature, which we enable (AGENT_CONSOLE_CODEX_ASK=0 turns it off). Returns undefined for
// requests that cannot be shown as a question card.
export function codexQuestion(method: string, params: any): CodexQuestionCard | undefined {
  if (method === "item/tool/requestUserInput" && Array.isArray(params?.questions))
    return userInputCard(params.questions);
  if (
    method === "mcpServer/elicitation/request" &&
    ["form", "openai/form"].includes(params?.mode) &&
    params.requestedSchema?.properties
  )
    return elicitationCard(params);
  return undefined;
}

function userInputCard(rawQuestions: any[]): CodexQuestionCard {
  const questions = cleanQuestions(
    rawQuestions.map((raw: any) => ({
      ...raw,
      options: raw.options ?? [],
      allowOther: raw.isOther || !raw.options?.length,
      secret: raw.isSecret,
    })),
  );
  return {
    questions,
    consumesNote: true,
    reply: (answer) => userInputReply(questions, answer),
  };
}

function userInputReply(questions: Question[], reply: QuestionReply) {
  if (reply.note !== undefined)
    return { answers: questions[0] ? { [questions[0].id]: { answers: [reply.note] } } : {} };
  if (reply.skip) return { answers: {} };
  return {
    answers: Object.fromEntries(
      questions
        .map((question) => [
          question.id,
          { answers: (reply.answers[question.id] ?? []).map(String).filter(Boolean) },
        ])
        .filter(([, entry]: any) => entry.answers.length),
    ),
  };
}

function enumValues(schema: any): string[] {
  return (
    schema?.enum ??
    schema?.oneOf?.map((option: any) => option.const) ??
    schema?.anyOf?.map((option: any) => option.const) ??
    []
  );
}

// Nested objects and large forms need a dedicated form renderer. Decline
// unsupported schemas instead of silently truncating or changing their types.
function unsupportedField(schema: any): boolean {
  if (!schema || !FORM_FIELD_TYPES.includes(schema.type)) return true;
  const isArray = schema.type === "array";
  if (isArray && schema.items?.type !== "string" && !Array.isArray(schema.items?.enum)) return true;
  return enumValues(isArray ? schema.items : schema).length > 20;
}

function elicitationCard(params: any): CodexQuestionCard | undefined {
  const fields = Object.entries<any>(params.requestedSchema.properties);
  const required = new Set<string>(params.requestedSchema.required ?? []);
  if (!fields.length || fields.length > 10 || fields.some(([, schema]) => unsupportedField(schema)))
    return undefined;
  const questions = cleanQuestions(
    fields.map(([key, schema], index) =>
      fieldQuestion(params, key, schema, index, required.has(key)),
    ),
  );
  return {
    questions,
    consumesNote: false,
    reply: (answer) => elicitationReply(fields, required, answer),
  };
}

function fieldQuestion(params: any, key: string, schema: any, index: number, isRequired: boolean) {
  const multi = schema.type === "array";
  const values =
    schema.type === "boolean" ? ["Yes", "No"] : enumValues(multi ? schema.items : schema);
  const first = index === 0;
  return {
    id: key,
    header: first ? params.serverName : undefined,
    question:
      [first ? params.message : "", schema.title || schema.description || key]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join("\n\n") + (isRequired ? "" : " (optional)"),
    options: values.map((value: string) => ({ label: String(value) })),
    multiSelect: multi,
    allowOther: !values.length,
  };
}

function elicitationReply(fields: [string, any][], required: Set<string>, reply: QuestionReply) {
  if (reply.skip || reply.note !== undefined)
    return { action: "decline", content: null, _meta: null };
  const content: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  for (const [key, schema] of fields) {
    const values = (reply.answers[key] ?? []).map(String).filter(Boolean);
    if (!values.length) {
      if (required.has(key)) throw new Error(`Please answer ${schema.title || key}.`);
      continue;
    }
    validateField(key, schema, values[0]);
    content[key] = fieldValue(schema, values);
  }
  return { action: "accept", content, _meta: null };
}

function validateField(key: string, schema: any, first: string): void {
  const label = schema.title || key;
  const numeric = schema.type === "number" || schema.type === "integer";
  if (
    numeric &&
    (!Number.isFinite(Number(first)) ||
      (schema.type === "integer" && !Number.isInteger(Number(first))))
  )
    throw new Error(`Enter a valid ${schema.type} for ${label}.`);
  if (schema.type === "boolean" && !["Yes", "No"].includes(first))
    throw new Error(`Choose Yes or No for ${label}.`);
}

function fieldValue(schema: any, values: string[]): unknown {
  if (schema.type === "array") return values;
  if (schema.type === "boolean") return values[0] === "Yes";
  if (schema.type === "number" || schema.type === "integer") return Number(values[0]);
  return values[0];
}


================
File: server/providers/codex-app-rpc.ts
================
import type { ExitInfo } from "./types.ts";

interface PendingRequest {
  resolve: (value: any) => void;
  reject: (error: Error) => void;
  timer: NodeJS.Timeout;
}

// JSON-RPC client half of the app-server connection: numbered requests, their pending replies and the fixed timeout.
export class CodexRpc {
  open = true;
  private serial = 0;
  private readonly pending = new Map<number, PendingRequest>();
  private readonly writeLine: (line: string) => void;

  constructor(writeLine: (line: string) => void) {
    this.writeLine = writeLine;
  }

  request(method: string, params: object): Promise<any> {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      if (!this.open) {
        reject(new Error("Codex connection closed."));
        return;
      }
      const requestId = ++this.serial;
      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
        this.pending.delete(requestId);
        reject(new Error(`Codex ${method} timed out.`));
      }, 30_000);
      this.pending.set(requestId, { resolve, reject, timer });
      this.writeLine(JSON.stringify({ id: requestId, method, params }));
    });
  }

  // A response line (id, no method) settles its waiting request; unknown ids are ignored.
  settle(response: any): void {
    const wait = this.pending.get(response.id);
    if (!wait) return;
    this.pending.delete(response.id);
    clearTimeout(wait.timer);
    if (response.error) wait.reject(new Error(response.error.message || "Codex request rejected."));
    else wait.resolve(response.result);
  }

  rejectAll(info: ExitInfo): void {
    for (const wait of this.pending.values()) {
      clearTimeout(wait.timer);
      wait.reject(new Error(`Codex connection closed (${info.code ?? info.signal}).`));
    }
    this.pending.clear();
  }
}

// Filtered excerpt: some source lines are omitted.
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { launch as sandboxLaunch } from "../automation/sandbox.ts";
import { retryEvent } from "../turn-timing.ts";
import { harnessInstructions, personaFor, personaInstructions } from "./brain.ts";
import { adaptItem } from "./codex-app-items.ts";
import { codexQuestion } from "./codex-app-questions.ts";
import { CodexRpc } from "./codex-app-rpc.ts";
import { SubagentTracker } from "./codex-app-subagents.ts";
import { codexRouting } from "./codex-routing.ts";
import { consoleCodexMcpArgs } from "./console-mcp.mjs";
import { childEnv } from "./env.ts";
import { type Proc, parseJson, spawnLines } from "./proc.ts";
import {
  type AgentCtx,
  type Attachment,
  type ExitInfo,
  IMAGE_ONLY_PROMPT,
  type QuestionReply,
  type ThreadSpec,
} from "./types.ts";

interface PendingAsk {
  rpcId: any;
  consumesNote: boolean;
  reply: (answer: QuestionReply) => any;
}

type NotificationHandler = (session: CodexAppSession, params: any) => void;

// Parent-thread notifications by method; methods without an entry are ignored.
const NOTIFICATION_HANDLERS = new Map<string, NotificationHandler>([
  ["serverRequest/resolved", (session, params) => session.requestResolved(params)],
  ["turn/started", (session, params) => session.turnStarted(params)],
  ["turn/completed", (session, params) => session.turnCompleted(params)],
  ["item/started", (session, params) => session.itemEvent(params, false)],
  ["item/completed", (session, params) => session.itemEvent(params, true)],
// ...
  ["account/rateLimits/updated", (session, params) => session.rateLimitsUpdated(params)],
  ["error", (session, params) => session.errorNotified(params)],
]);

function hasEffort(spec: ThreadSpec): boolean {
  return Boolean(spec.effort && spec.effort !== "default");
}

function sandboxMode(mode: ThreadSpec["mode"]): string {
  if (mode === "bypassPermissions") return "danger-full-access";
  if (mode === "plan") return "read-only";
  return "workspace-write";
}

function threadParams(spec: ThreadSpec, threadId: string | undefined, brain: string | undefined) {
  return {
    ...(threadId ? { threadId, excludeTurns: true } : {}),
    cwd: spec.cwd,
    approvalPolicy: "never",
    sandbox: sandboxMode(spec.mode),
    ...(spec.model !== "default" ? { model: spec.model } : {}),
    ...(hasEffort(spec) ? { config: { model_reasoning_effort: spec.effort } } : {}),
    ...(brain ? { developerInstructions: brain } : {}),
  };
}

function turnInput(text: string, images: Attachment[]) {
  return [
    { type: "text", text: text.trim() ? text : IMAGE_ONLY_PROMPT },
    ...images.map((image) => ({ type: "localImage", path: image.path })),
  ];
}

function rateWindow(window: any) {
  return (
    window && {
      used_percent: window.usedPercent,
      window_minutes: window.windowDurationMins,
      resets_at: window.resetsAt,
    }
  );
}

function errorKind(info: any) {
  return info && typeof info === "object" ? Object.keys(info)[0] : info;
}

// ...
  return {
// ...
    input_tokens: Math.max(0, total.inputTokens - baseline.inputTokens),
    cached_input_tokens: Math.max(0, total.cachedInputTokens - baseline.cachedInputTokens),
    output_tokens: Math.max(0, total.outputTokens - baseline.outputTokens),
    ...(typeof total.reasoningOutputTokens === "number"
      ? {
          reasoning_output_tokens: Math.max(
            0,
            total.reasoningOutputTokens - (baseline.reasoningOutputTokens || 0),
          ),
        }
      : {}),
  };
}

// Shared state of one app-server process: the parent thread, the running operation, questions, RPC and subagents.
export class CodexAppSession {
  readonly spec: ThreadSpec;
  readonly ctx: AgentCtx;
  readonly translate: (row: any) => void;
  readonly rpc: CodexRpc;
  readonly subagents: SubagentTracker;
  threadId: string | undefined;
  active: string | undefined;
  operation: "turn" | "compact" | undefined;
  compactConfirmed = false;
  compactTimer: NodeJS.Timeout | undefined;
  total: any;
  baseline: any;
  readonly asks = new Map<string, PendingAsk>(); // pending questions by canonical id
  // App-server request ids restart with each child process. History persists
  // across those restarts, so canonical ids also need a process-specific prefix.
  readonly questionPrefix = `codex-${randomUUID()}-`;
  proc!: Proc;
  ready!: Promise<void>;
  starting: Promise<any> | undefined;

  constructor(spec: ThreadSpec, ctx: AgentCtx, translate: (row: any) => void) {
    this.spec = spec;
    this.ctx = ctx;
    this.translate = translate;
    this.threadId = spec.sessionId;
    this.rpc = new CodexRpc((line) => this.proc.write(line));
    this.subagents = new SubagentTracker(ctx);
  }

  start(): void {
    const askArgs =
      process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_CODEX_ASK === "0"
        ? []
        : ["--enable", "default_mode_request_user_input"];
    const routing = codexRouting({ ...childEnv(), AGENT_CONSOLE_THREAD: this.spec.id });
    const launch = sandboxLaunch(
      this.spec,
      "codex",
      [
        "app-server",
        "-c",
        "analytics.enabled=false",
        "-c",
        "feedback.enabled=false",
        ...askArgs,
        ...routing.args,
        ...consoleCodexMcpArgs(),
      ],
      routing.env,
    );
    this.proc = spawnLines(
      "codex",
      launch.command,
      launch.args,
      { cwd: this.spec.cwd, env: launch.env },
      { ...this.ctx, exited: (info) => this.exited(info) },
      (line) => this.handleLine(line),
    );
    this.ready = this.connect(askArgs.length > 0);
    void this.ready.catch((error) => this.failed(error));
  }

  get alive(): boolean {
    return this.rpc.open && !this.proc.aborted;
  }

  finish(error?: string): void {
    if (!this.operation) return;
    const wasCompact = this.operation === "compact";
    this.operation = undefined;
    this.active = undefined;
    clearTimeout(this.compactTimer);
    let message = error;
    if (wasCompact && !this.compactConfirmed && !message)
      message = "Codex did not confirm context compaction.";
    this.translate(
      message ? { type: "turn.failed", error: { message } } : { type: "turn.completed" },
    );
  }

  failed(error: any): void {
    this.finish(String(error?.message || error));
    this.proc.kill();
  }

  cancelQuestions(): void {
    for (const askId of this.asks.keys())
      this.ctx.event({ type: "question_answer", id: askId, cancelled: true });
    this.asks.clear();
  }

  private exited(info: ExitInfo): void {
    this.rpc.open = false;
    this.cancelQuestions();
    this.rpc.rejectAll(info);
    clearTimeout(this.compactTimer);
    this.ctx.exited(info);
  }

  private async connect(experimentalApi: boolean): Promise<void> {
    await this.rpc.request("initialize", {
      clientInfo: { name: "agent_console", title: "Agent Console", version: "1.0.0" },
      capabilities: { experimentalApi },
    });
    this.proc.write(JSON.stringify({ method: "initialized", params: {} }));
    // Codex custom agents are spawn-only, so the main thread (never a subagent) gets the persona body as developer instructions.
    const brain = [personaInstructions(personaFor(this.spec)), await harnessInstructions()]
      .filter(Boolean)
      .join("\n\n");
    const result = await this.rpc.request(
      this.threadId ? "thread/resume" : "thread/start",
      threadParams(this.spec, this.threadId, brain),
    );
    this.threadId = result.thread.id;
    this.translate({
      type: "thread.started",
      thread_id: this.threadId,
      ...(typeof result.model === "string" ? { model: result.model } : {}),
    });
  }

  handleLine(line: string): void {
    const message = parseJson(line);
    if (!message) return;
    if (message.id != null && !message.method) {
      this.rpc.settle(message);
      return;
    }
    if (message.id != null && message.method) {
      this.serverRequest(message);
      return;
    }
    const params = message.params || {};
    if (params.threadId && params.threadId !== this.threadId) {
      this.subagents.onChildNotification(message.method, params, Boolean(this.operation));
      return;
    }
    NOTIFICATION_HANDLERS.get(message.method)?.(this, params);
  }

  // Questions become cards; the answer is whatever the user picks. This adapter never grants an approval.
  private serverRequest(message: any): void {
    const card = codexQuestion(message.method, message.params);
    if (card?.questions.length) {
      const askId = `${this.questionPrefix}${message.id}`;
      this.asks.set(askId, {
        rpcId: message.id,
        consumesNote: card.consumesNote,
        reply: card.reply,
      });
      this.ctx.event({ type: "question", id: askId, questions: card.questions, ts: Date.now() });
      return;
    }
    this.proc.write(
      JSON.stringify({
        id: message.id,
        error: {
          code: -32601,
          message:
            "This Console cannot answer interactive requests. Use a message to provide guidance.",
        },
      }),
    );
    this.ctx.event({
      type: "console_error",
      text: `Codex requested ${message.method}; interactive response is unavailable.`,
    });
  }

  requestResolved(params: any): void {
    const askId = `${this.questionPrefix}${params.requestId}`;
    if (this.asks.delete(askId))
      this.ctx.event({ type: "question_answer", id: askId, cancelled: true });
  }

  turnStarted(params: any): void {
    this.active = params.turn?.id;
    this.baseline = this.total;
    this.translate({ type: "turn.started" });
  }

  turnCompleted(params: any): void {
    if (this.active && params.turn?.id && this.active !== params.turn.id) return;
    this.cancelQuestions();
    this.finish(
      params.turn?.status === "completed"
        ? undefined
        : params.turn?.error?.message || `Codex turn ${params.turn?.status || "failed"}.`,
    );
    this.subagents.wakeUnseen();
  }

  itemEvent(params: any, done: boolean): void {
    const item = params.item || {};
    this.subagents.noteParentActivity(item.type);
    if (item.type === "subAgentActivity") {
      this.subagents.onActivity(item, done, Boolean(this.operation));
      return;
    }
    if (item.type === "contextCompaction" && done) {
      this.compactConfirmed = true;
      this.ctx.event({ type: "system", subtype: "compact_boundary" });
      return;
    }
    const row = adaptItem(item, done);
    if (row) this.translate(row);
    else if (done && item.type === "plan")
      this.ctx.event({
        type: "assistant",
        message: { content: [{ type: "text", text: item.text }] },
      });
  }

// ...
    if (last)
      this.ctx.event({
// ...
        used: last.totalTokens,
// ...
        source: "codex/last_request",
      });
// ...
    if (!this.countsTowardTurn(params.turnId) || !this.total || !last) return;
    const total = this.total;
    this.baseline ||= Object.fromEntries(
      Object.keys(total).map((key) => [key, Math.max(0, total[key] - (last[key] || 0))]),
    );
// ...
  }

  private countsTowardTurn(turnId: string | undefined): boolean {
    return Boolean(this.operation === "turn" && this.active && (!turnId || turnId === this.active));
  }

  rateLimitsUpdated(params: any): void {
    this.ctx.event({
// ...
      rateLimits: {
        primary: rateWindow(params.rateLimits?.primary),
        secondary: rateWindow(params.rateLimits?.secondary),
      },
    });
  }

  errorNotified(params: any): void {
    const info = params.error?.codexErrorInfo;
    const kind = errorKind(info);
    if (params.willRetry) {
      this.ctx.event(
        retryEvent({
          status: kind && info?.[kind]?.httpStatusCode,
          error: typeof kind === "string" ? kind : undefined,
          message: String(params.error?.message ?? ""),
        }),
      );
      return;
    }
    this.ctx.event({
      type: "console_error",
      text: params.error?.message || "Codex error.",
      ...(typeof kind === "string" ? { code: kind } : {}),
    });
  }

  send(text: string, images: Attachment[]): void {
    this.operation = "turn";
    this.baseline = this.total;
    this.ctx.event({
      type: "run_settings",
      model: this.spec.model,
      effort: this.spec.effort || "default",
      source: this.spec.model === "default" ? "Codex CLI configuration" : "Thread override",
    });
    this.starting = this.ready
      .then(() =>
        this.rpc.request("turn/start", {
          threadId: this.threadId,
          input: turnInput(text, images),
          ...(hasEffort(this.spec) ? { effort: this.spec.effort } : {}),
        }),
      )
      .then((result) => {
        if (this.operation === "turn") this.active = result.turn.id;
      });
    void this.starting.catch((error) => this.failed(error));
  }

  async steer(text: string, images: Attachment[]): Promise<void> {
    await this.ready;
    await this.starting;
    if (!this.active || this.operation !== "turn")
      throw new Error("Codex turn finished before steering was accepted.");
    const expected = this.active;
    const result = await this.rpc.request("turn/steer", {
      threadId: this.threadId,
      expectedTurnId: expected,
      input: turnInput(text, images),
    });
    if (result.turnId !== expected)
      throw new Error("Codex did not confirm steering the active turn.");
  }

  compact(instructions: string): void {
    if (instructions) throw new Error("Codex /compact does not accept extra instructions.");
    if (!this.spec.sessionId) throw new Error("Send a message before compacting this thread.");
    this.operation = "compact";
    this.compactConfirmed = false;
    this.compactTimer = setTimeout(
      () => this.failed(new Error("Context compaction timed out.")),
      180_000,
    );
    void this.ready
      .then(() => this.rpc.request("thread/compact/start", { threadId: this.threadId }))
      .catch((error) => this.failed(error));
  }

  answer(askId: string, reply: QuestionReply): boolean {
    const pendingAsk = this.asks.get(askId);
    if (!pendingAsk || !this.rpc.open)
      throw new Error("This question is no longer waiting for an answer.");
    const result = pendingAsk.reply(reply);
    this.proc.write(JSON.stringify({ id: pendingAsk.rpcId, result }));
    this.asks.delete(askId);
    return reply.note !== undefined && pendingAsk.consumesNote;
  }

  stop(hard: boolean): void {
    this.operation = undefined;
    clearTimeout(this.compactTimer);
    this.cancelQuestions();
    if (hard) this.proc.kill();
    else this.proc.abort();
  }
}

// Filtered excerpt: some source lines are omitted.
import { codexTranslator } from "./codex.ts";
import { adaptItem } from "./codex-app-items.ts";
import type { AgentCtx } from "./types.ts";

interface ChildAgent {
  path: string;
  call: string;
  text: string;
  translate: (row: any) => void;
  done?: boolean;
  failed?: string;
}

const PARENT_ACTIVITY_TYPES = ["agentMessage", "reasoning", "commandExecution", "mcpToolCall"];

// ...
function childFailure(method: string, params: any): string {
  if (method === "turn/completed" && ["failed", "interrupted"].includes(params.turn?.status))
    return params.turn?.error?.message || `turn ${params.turn.status}`;
  if (method === "error" && !params.willRetry) return params.error?.message || "Codex error.";
  return "";
}

// Subagents by thread id. Codex does not start a parent turn when one finishes after the parent's turn ended, so we do.
export class SubagentTracker {
  readonly children = new Map<string, ChildAgent>();
  heartbeat = 0; // child progress never reaches ctx.event otherwise; the stale-background sweep needs proof of life
  unseen: string[] = []; // finished while the parent turn ran, before the parent model looked again
  private readonly ctx: AgentCtx;

  constructor(ctx: AgentCtx) {
    this.ctx = ctx;
  }

  backgroundChanged(): void {
    this.ctx.event({
      type: "system",
      subtype: "background_tasks_changed",
      tasks: [...this.children]
        .filter(([, child]) => !child.done)
        .map(([threadId, child]) => ({
          task_id: threadId,
          tool_use_id: child.call,
          description: child.path,
        })),
    });
  }

  doneText(threadId: string): string {
    const child = this.children.get(threadId)!;
    if (child.failed) return `[Background subagent ${child.path} failed: ${child.failed}]`;
    return `[Background subagent ${child.path} finished. Its final message:]\n${child.text.trim() || "(no message)"}`;
  }

  // A notification for another thread than the parent session.
  onChildNotification(method: string, params: any, busy: boolean): void {
    const child = this.children.get(params.threadId);
    if (!child) return;
    if (!child.done && Date.now() - this.heartbeat > 60_000) {
      this.heartbeat = Date.now();
      this.ctx.event({ type: "system", subtype: "task_updated", task_id: params.threadId });
    }
    if (
      method === "item/completed" &&
      params.item?.type === "agentMessage" &&
      typeof params.item.text === "string"
    )
      child.text = params.item.text;
    // Child output reaches the parent card through the child's own translator, tagged with the spawning call. Turn lifecycle is never fed to it.
    if (!child.done && (method === "item/started" || method === "item/completed"))
      forwardChildItem(child, params.item || {}, method === "item/completed");
    const failure = childFailure(method, params);
    if (!child.done && failure) {
      child.done = true;
      child.failed = failure.slice(0, 300);
      this.ctx.event({
        type: "system",
        subtype: "task_notification",
        task_id: params.threadId,
        tool_use_id: child.call,
        status: "failed",
        summary: child.failed,
      });
      this.settled(params.threadId, busy);
    }
  }

  // The parent model ran after the result arrived.
  noteParentActivity(itemType: string): void {
    if (
      itemType !== "subAgentActivity" &&
      this.unseen.length &&
      PARENT_ACTIVITY_TYPES.includes(itemType)
    )
      this.unseen = [];
  }

  // A subAgentActivity item in the parent thread.
  onActivity(item: any, done: boolean, busy: boolean): void {
    if (!done || !item.agentThreadId) return;
    const child = this.children.get(item.agentThreadId);
    if (item.kind === "started" && !this.children.has(item.agentThreadId)) this.started(item);
    else if (item.kind === "completed" && child && !child.done)
      this.completed(item.agentThreadId, child, busy);
  }

  wakeUnseen(): void {
    for (const threadId of this.unseen.splice(0))
      this.ctx.event({ type: "subagent_wake", text: this.doneText(threadId) });
  }

  private started(item: any): void {
    const ctx = this.ctx;
    this.children.set(item.agentThreadId, {
      path: item.agentPath || "subagent",
      call: item.id,
      text: "",
      translate: codexTranslator((event) => ctx.event({ ...event, parent_tool_use_id: item.id })),
    });
    ctx.event({
      type: "assistant",
      message: {
        role: "assistant",
        content: [
          {
            type: "tool_use",
            id: item.id,
            name: "Agent",
            input: { description: item.agentPath || "Subagent" },
          },
        ],
      },
    });
    ctx.event({
      type: "user",
      message: {
        role: "user",
        content: [
          {
            type: "tool_result",
            tool_use_id: item.id,
            content: "Started in the background.",
          },
        ],
      },
    });
    ctx.event({
      type: "system",
      subtype: "task_started",
      task_id: item.agentThreadId,
      tool_use_id: item.id,
      task_type: "local_agent",
      is_backgrounded: true,
      description: item.agentPath,
    });
    this.backgroundChanged();
  }

  private completed(threadId: string, child: ChildAgent, busy: boolean): void {
    child.done = true;
    this.ctx.event({
      type: "system",
      subtype: "task_notification",
      task_id: threadId,
      tool_use_id: child.call,
      status: "completed",
      summary: child.text.slice(0, 300),
    });
    this.settled(threadId, busy);
  }

  private settled(threadId: string, busy: boolean): void {
    this.backgroundChanged();
    if (busy) this.unseen.push(threadId);
    else this.ctx.event({ type: "subagent_wake", text: this.doneText(threadId) });
  }
}

function forwardChildItem(child: ChildAgent, item: any, done: boolean): void {
  const row = adaptItem(item, done);
  if (row)
    child.translate(
      row.item?.questions ? { ...row, item: { ...row.item, questions: undefined } } : row,
    ); // a child cannot raise a console question card
}

import { CodexAppSession } from "./codex-app-session.ts";
import type { Agent, AgentCtx, ThreadSpec } from "./types.ts";

export { codexQuestion } from "./codex-app-questions.ts";

// Keep the owning app-server alive: turn/steer must reach the process running the turn.
export function startCodexApp(
  spec: ThreadSpec,
  ctx: AgentCtx,
  translate: (row: any) => void,
): Agent {
  const session = new CodexAppSession(spec, ctx, translate);
  session.start();
  return {
    get alive() {
      return session.alive;
    },
    send(text, images = []) {
      session.send(text, images);
    },
    steer(text, images = []) {
      return session.steer(text, images);
    },
    compact(instructions = "") {
      session.compact(instructions);
    },
    answer(askId, reply) {
      return session.answer(askId, reply);
    },
    abort() {
      session.stop(false);
    },
    kill() {
      session.stop(true);
    },
  };
}

// exec --json carries the Responses API's snake_case action (older CLIs may omit it).
// Never treat the legacy query field as a search when an explicit different action exists.
function isNonEmpty(value: unknown): value is string {
  return typeof value === "string" && !!value.trim();
}

function webSearchInput(item: any, action: any): Record<string, any> {
  const input: Record<string, any> = {};
  if (isNonEmpty(action.query)) input.query = action.query;
  else if (isNonEmpty(item.query)) input.query = item.query;
  if (Array.isArray(action.queries) && action.queries.some(isNonEmpty))
    input.queries = action.queries.filter(isNonEmpty);
  if (!input.query && !input.queries) input.description = "Search query not reported by Codex";
  return input;
}

function webFetchInput(action: any): Record<string, any> {
  if (isNonEmpty(action.url)) return { url: action.url };
  return { description: "Page URL not reported by Codex" };
}

function webFindInput(action: any): Record<string, any> {
  const input: Record<string, any> = {};
  if (isNonEmpty(action.url)) input.url = action.url;
  if (isNonEmpty(action.pattern)) input.pattern = action.pattern;
  if (!input.pattern) input.description = "Find pattern not reported by Codex";
  return input;
}

export function codexWebTool(item: any) {
  const action = item.action ?? {};
  if (action.type === "search" || (!action.type && isNonEmpty(item.query)))
    return { name: "WebSearch", input: webSearchInput(item, action) };
  if (["open_page", "openPage"].includes(action.type))
    return { name: "WebFetch", input: webFetchInput(action) };
  if (["find_in_page", "findInPage"].includes(action.type))
    return { name: "WebFind", input: webFindInput(action) };
  return { name: "WebBrowse", input: { description: "Web action details not reported by Codex" } };
}

File: server/providers/codex.ts

// Filtered excerpt: some source lines are omitted.
import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import fs from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import { startCodexApp } from "./codex-app.ts";
import { codexWebTool } from "./codex-web.ts";
import {
  type Agent,
  type AgentCtx,
  cleanQuestions,
  clip,
  type Mode,
  type Provider,
  type ThreadSpec,
} from "./types.ts";

// Persistent Codex app-server owns each active session and accepts native mid-turn steering.
// The pure exec translator is shared with the app-server item adapter and historical fixtures.

// codex runs everything as `/usr/bin/zsh -lc '<command>'`; show just the command
export const unwrapShell = (cmd: unknown): string => {
  const c = String(cmd ?? "");
  const m = c.match(/^\S*\/(?:ba|z|da)?sh -lc (['"])([\s\S]*)\1$/);
  if (!m) return c;
  return m[1] === "'" ? m[2].replaceAll("'\\''", "'") : m[2].replaceAll('\\"', '"');
};

const asst = (b: any) => ({ type: "assistant", message: { role: "assistant", content: [b] } });
const result = (id: string, text: string, isError: boolean) => ({
  type: "user",
  message: {
    role: "user",
    content: [{ type: "tool_result", tool_use_id: id, content: clip(text), is_error: isError }],
  },
});

// codex reports harmless ~/.codex/config.toml warnings as "error" items before every turn; the user can't act on them from here
const CONFIG_WARNING = /^Codex is ignoring \d+ unrecognized configuration setting/;

type CodexState = {
  emit: (event: any) => void;
  started: number;
  begun: Set<string>;
  web: Map<string, string>;
  asked: Set<string>;
};

function codexFinish(state: CodexState, failure?: string) {
  if (failure) state.emit({ type: "console_error", text: failure });
  state.emit({
    type: "result",
    subtype: failure ? "error" : "success",
    is_error: !!failure,
    duration_ms: state.started ? Date.now() - state.started : 0,
  });
  state.started = 0;
  state.begun.clear();
  state.web.clear();
}

function codexTool(state: CodexState, toolId: string, name: string, input: Record<string, any>) {
  if (state.begun.has(toolId)) return;
  state.begun.add(toolId);
  state.emit(asst({ type: "tool_use", id: toolId, name, input }));
}

function asyncQuestions(item: any) {
  const raw = Array.isArray(item.questions) ? item.questions : [];
  return cleanQuestions(
    raw
      .map((entry: any, index: number) => ({
        id: String(index),
        question: entry?.title,
        options: Array.isArray(entry?.options)
          ? entry.options.map((label: string) => ({ label }))
          : [],
        allowOther: true,
      }))
      .filter((entry: any) => typeof entry.question === "string" && entry.question.trim()),
  );
}

function codexAgentMessage(state: CodexState, item: any, done: boolean) {
  if (!done) return;
  // request_user_input_async is an agentMessage with question metadata,
  // not a blocking app-server request. Its text is only a fallback for text clients.
  const questions = asyncQuestions(item);
  if (!questions.length) {
    state.emit(asst({ type: "text", text: String(item.text ?? "") }));
    return;
  }
  const questionId = `codex-async-${item.id || randomUUID()}`;
  if (state.asked.has(questionId)) return;
  state.asked.add(questionId);
  state.emit({ type: "question", id: questionId, async: true, questions, ts: Date.now() });
}

function codexReasoning(state: CodexState, item: any, done: boolean) {
  if (done && item.text) state.emit(asst({ type: "thinking", thinking: String(item.text) }));
}

function codexCommand(state: CodexState, item: any, done: boolean) {
  codexTool(state, item.id, "Bash", {
    command: unwrapShell(item.command),
    ...(typeof item.cwd === "string" ? { cwd: item.cwd } : {}),
  });
  if (!done) return;
  state.emit(
    result(
      item.id,
      String(item.aggregated_output ?? ""),
      item.status === "failed" || (item.exit_code ?? 0) !== 0,
    ),
  );
}

function codexFileChange(state: CodexState, item: any, done: boolean) {
  const changes = item.changes ?? [];
  codexTool(state, item.id, "Edit", { file_path: changes[0]?.path, changes });
  if (!done) return;
  state.emit(
    result(
      item.id,
      changes.map((change: any) => `${change.kind} ${change.path}`).join("\n") || "no changes",
      item.status === "failed",
    ),
  );
}

function codexMcpCall(state: CodexState, item: any, done: boolean) {
  codexTool(state, item.id, `${item.server}:${item.tool}`, item.arguments ?? {});
  if (!done) return;
  state.emit(
    result(
      item.id,
      JSON.stringify(item.result ?? item.error ?? "", null, 1),
      item.status === "failed",
    ),
  );
}

function webOutput(item: any, failed: boolean) {
  const reported = item.error?.message ?? item.error;
  if (reported !== undefined && reported !== null) return reported;
  if (failed) return `Web action ${item.status}`;
  return item.results ? JSON.stringify(item.results, null, 2) : "";
}

function codexWebSearch(state: CodexState, item: any, done: boolean) {
  const details = codexWebTool(item);
  const signature = JSON.stringify(details);
  if (!state.begun.has(item.id)) codexTool(state, item.id, details.name, details.input);
  else if (state.web.get(item.id) !== signature)
    state.emit({ type: "tool_update", tool_use_id: item.id, ...details });
  state.web.set(item.id, signature);
  if (!done) return;
  const failed = ["failed", "incomplete", "cancelled"].includes(item.status) || !!item.error;
  state.emit(result(item.id, String(webOutput(item, failed)), failed));
}

function codexItemError(state: CodexState, item: any, done: boolean) {
  if (done && !CONFIG_WARNING.test(String(item.message)))
    state.emit({ type: "console_error", text: String(item.message ?? "error") });
}

const CODEX_ITEM_HANDLERS: Record<string, (state: CodexState, item: any, done: boolean) => void> =
  Object.assign(Object.create(null), {
    agent_message: codexAgentMessage,
    reasoning: codexReasoning,
    command_execution: codexCommand,
    file_change: codexFileChange,
    mcp_tool_call: codexMcpCall,
    web_search: codexWebSearch,
    error: codexItemError,
  });

function codexItemEvent(state: CodexState, rec: any) {
  const item = rec.item ?? {};
  CODEX_ITEM_HANDLERS[item.type]?.(state, item, rec.type === "item.completed");
}

const CODEX_HANDLERS: Record<string, (state: CodexState, rec: any) => void> = Object.assign(
  Object.create(null),
  {
    "thread.started": (state: CodexState, rec: any) =>
      state.emit({
        type: "system",
        subtype: "init",
        session_id: rec.thread_id,
        ...(rec.model ? { model: rec.model } : {}),
      }), // resolved model id for turn timing
    "turn.started": (state: CodexState) => {
      state.started ||= Date.now();
      state.emit({ type: "stream_event", event: { type: "message_start" } });
    },
    "item.started": codexItemEvent,
    "item.updated": codexItemEvent,
    "item.completed": codexItemEvent,
    "turn.completed": (state: CodexState, rec: any) => {
// ...
      codexFinish(state);
    },
    "turn.failed": (state: CodexState, rec: any) =>
      codexFinish(state, String(rec.error?.message ?? "turn failed")),
    error: (state: CodexState, rec: any) => {
      if (!state.started)
        state.emit({ type: "console_error", text: String(rec.message ?? "error") });
    },
  },
);

export function codexTranslator(emit: (event: any) => void) {
  const state: CodexState = {
    emit,
    started: 0,
    begun: new Set<string>(),
    web: new Map<string, string>(),
    asked: new Set<string>(),
  };
  return (rec: any) => {
    CODEX_HANDLERS[rec?.type]?.(state, rec);
  };
}

const loggedIn = () =>
  fs.existsSync(
    path.join(process.env.CODEX_HOME || path.join(os.homedir(), ".codex"), "auth.json"),
  );

// Codex's Plan and Accept-edits modes rely on its bubblewrap sandbox, which needs unprivileged user namespaces. Many containers
// (this one included) forbid them, and every command then fails with "bwrap: No permissions to create a new namespace".
let sandbox: boolean | undefined;
const sandboxWorks = () =>
  (sandbox ??= spawnSync("unshare", ["--user", "true"], { stdio: "ignore" }).status === 0);

// Read only the public model metadata from the CLI's account-specific cache. Never expose prompts or auth data.
export function codexModels() {
  const home = process.env.CODEX_HOME || path.join(os.homedir(), ".codex");
  let models: any[] = [];
  try {
    models = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(home, "models_cache.json"), "utf8")).models || [];
  } catch {}
  const details: Record<
    string,
    { label: string; description?: string; efforts: string[]; defaultEffort?: string }
  > = {};
  details.default = {
    label: "CLI default",
    description:
      "Inherits Codex configuration, project overrides and CLI defaults. Select a model to pin it for this thread.",
    efforts: [],
  };
  for (const m of models) {
    if (m.visibility !== "list" || typeof m.slug !== "string") continue;
    details[m.slug] = {
      label: m.display_name || m.slug,
      description: m.description,
      efforts: (m.supported_reasoning_levels || [])
        .map((e: any) => e.effort)
        .filter((e: any) => typeof e === "string"),
      defaultEffort: m.default_reasoning_level,
    };
  }
  for (const id of (process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_CODEX_MODELS || "")
    .split(",")
    .map((s) => s.trim())
    .filter(Boolean)) {
    details[id] ||= {
      label: id,
      description: "Configured model; supported effort levels are unknown.",
      efforts: [],
    };
  }
  const known = Object.entries(details)
    .filter(([id, m]) => id !== "default" && m.efforts.length)
    .map(([, m]) => m.efforts);
  details.default.efforts = known.length
    ? known[0].filter((e) => known.every((levels) => levels.includes(e)))
    : [];
  return details;
}

export const codex: Provider = {
  id: "codex",
  label: "Codex",
  steer: true,
  get modes(): Mode[] {
    return sandboxWorks() ? ["plan", "acceptEdits", "bypassPermissions"] : ["bypassPermissions"];
  },
  get defaultMode(): Mode {
    return sandboxWorks() ? "acceptEdits" : "bypassPermissions";
  },
  get note(): string {
    return sandboxWorks()
      ? "Uses your ChatGPT login. Plan is a read-only sandbox, Accept edits is workspace-write."
      : "Uses your ChatGPT login. This machine cannot run Codex's sandbox (no user namespaces), so only Bypass is available.";
  },
  // "default" = whatever ~/.codex/config.toml selects
  models: () => Object.keys(codexModels()),
  modelDetails: codexModels,
  defaultModel: () => "default",
  status: () =>
    loggedIn()
      ? { ok: true }
      : { ok: false, hint: "Run `codex login --device-auth` in a terminal." },
  start(t: ThreadSpec, ctx: AgentCtx): Agent {
    return startCodexApp(t, ctx, codexTranslator(ctx.event));
  },
};

File: server/providers/console-mcp.mjs

// Filtered excerpt: some source lines are omitted.
// Minimal newline-delimited stdio MCP bridge. No network listener or credentials in arguments.
import { createInterface } from "node:readline";
import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from "node:url";
import { callTool, TOOLS } from "./console-tools.mjs";

export const SESSION_INSTRUCTIONS = [
  "# Agent Console",
  "When you need a decision or input from the user, use your structured question tool (AskUserQuestion, request_user_input or ask_user) instead of asking in chat. It shows an answer card in Agent Console. Use plain chat only for open-ended discussion or when no such tool exists.",
  "Use thread_list and thread_send to contact other Agent Console threads.",
// ...
].join("\n");

// The tool surface is frozen for a thread's whole life: any change busts every live thread's prompt cache (test/tool-surface-freeze.ts).
const exposed = (name) => name === "thread_list" || name === "thread_send";

export function consoleMcpServer() {
  if (!process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_TOOL_URL || !process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_TOOL_TOKEN)
    return undefined;
  return { command: process.execPath, args: [fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)] };
}

export function consoleCodexMcpArgs() {
  const server = consoleMcpServer();
  if (!server) return [];
  // TOML accepts JSON string/array literals. Credentials remain inherited environment values.
  return [
    "-c",
    `mcp_servers.agent_console.command=${JSON.stringify(server.command)}`,
    "-c",
    `mcp_servers.agent_console.args=${JSON.stringify(server.args)}`,
    "-c",
    'mcp_servers.agent_console.env_vars=["AGENT_CONSOLE_TOOL_URL","AGENT_CONSOLE_TOOL_TOKEN","AGENT_CONSOLE_THREAD"]',
  ];
}

export async function handleMcp(request) {
  if (request.id === undefined) return undefined;
  const result = (value) => ({ jsonrpc: "2.0", id: request.id, result: value });
  const error = (code, message) => ({ jsonrpc: "2.0", id: request.id, error: { code, message } });
  switch (request.method) {
    case "initialize":
      return result({
        protocolVersion: "2024-11-05",
        capabilities: { tools: {} },
        serverInfo: { name: "agent-console-sessions", version: "1.0.0" },
        instructions: SESSION_INSTRUCTIONS,
      });
    case "ping":
      return result({});
    case "tools/list":
      return result({
        tools: TOOLS.filter((t) => exposed(t.name)).map((t) => ({
          name: t.name,
          description: t.description,
          inputSchema: t.parameters,
          annotations: {
            readOnlyHint: t.name !== "thread_send",
            destructiveHint: false,
          },
        })),
      });
    case "tools/call": {
      const name = request.params?.name;
      if (!TOOLS.some((t) => t.name === name && exposed(name)))
        return error(-32602, "Unknown session tool.");
      const text = await callTool(name, request.params?.arguments, AbortSignal.timeout(30_000));
      return result({
        content: [{ type: "text", text }],
        ...(text.startsWith("Error:") ? { isError: true } : {}),
      });
    }
    default:
      return error(-32601, "Method not found.");
  }
}

if (process.argv[1] && pathToFileURL(process.argv[1]).href === import.meta.url) {
  const lines = createInterface({ input: process.stdin, crlfDelay: Infinity });
  lines.on("line", async (line) => {
    try {
      const reply = await handleMcp(JSON.parse(line));
      if (reply) process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(reply) + "\n");
    } catch {
      process.stdout.write(
        JSON.stringify({
          jsonrpc: "2.0",
          id: null,
          error: { code: -32700, message: "Invalid JSON-RPC request." },
        }) + "\n",
      );
    }
  });
}

export async function callTool(name, params, signal) {
  const url = process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_TOOL_URL,
    token = process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_TOOL_TOKEN;
  if (!url || !token) return "Agent Console is not reachable from this session.";
  try {
    const r = await fetch(`${url}/${name}`, {
      method: "POST",
      signal,
      headers: {
        "content-type": "application/json",
        "x-agent-console-tool": token,
        "x-agent-console-thread": process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_THREAD || "",
      },
      body: JSON.stringify(params ?? {}),
    });
    const body = await r.json().catch(() => ({}));
    return r.ok
      ? typeof body === "string"
        ? body
        : JSON.stringify(body, null, 1)
      : `Error: ${body.error || r.status}`;
  } catch (e) {
    return `Error: Agent Console did not answer (${e?.message || e}).`;
  }
}

// ...
      ...tool,
      async execute(_toolCallId, params, signal) {
        return { content: [{ type: "text", text: await callTool(tool.name, params, signal) }] };
      },
    });
}
