// Filtered excerpt: some source lines are omitted.
import fs from "node:fs";

// ...
// a native byte search finds the few init and result lines among megabytes of tool output.
export function linesWith(buf: Buffer, needles: string[]): string[] {
  const starts = new Set<number>();
  for (const needle of needles) {
    for (let at = buf.indexOf(needle); at !== -1; ) {
      const start = buf.lastIndexOf(10, at) + 1;
      starts.add(start);
      const end = buf.indexOf(10, at);
      if (end === -1) break;
      at = buf.indexOf(needle, end);
    }
  }
  return [...starts]
    .sort((a, b) => a - b)
    .map((start) => {
      const end = buf.indexOf(10, start);
      return buf.toString("utf8", start, end === -1 ? buf.length : end);
    });
}

// Byte offsets, not parsed transcripts: retain a small index and read only the requested page.
// Extend indexes as JSONL grows; replacing/truncating a log invalidates its index.
export class HistoryIndex {
  private files = new Map<string, { ino: number; size: number; offsets: number[] }>();
  private extend(file: string, fd: number) {
    const stat = fs.fstatSync(fd);
    let index = this.files.get(file);
    if (!index || index.ino !== stat.ino || index.size > stat.size)
      index = { ino: stat.ino, size: 0, offsets: [0] };
    if (index.size < stat.size) {
      const chunk = Buffer.alloc(64 * 1024);
      let pos = index.size;
      while (pos < stat.size) {
        const n = fs.readSync(fd, chunk, 0, Math.min(chunk.length, stat.size - pos), pos);
        if (!n) break;
        for (let i = chunk.indexOf(10); i !== -1 && i < n; i = chunk.indexOf(10, i + 1))
          index.offsets.push(pos + i + 1);
        pos += n;
      }
      index.size = pos;
    }
    this.files.delete(file);
    this.files.set(file, index);
    if (this.files.size > 64) this.files.delete(this.files.keys().next().value!);
    return index;
  }
  private withFile<T>(file: string, empty: T, fn: (fd: number, offsets: number[]) => T): T {
    let fd: number;
    try {
      fd = fs.openSync(file, "r");
    } catch {
      return empty;
    }
    try {
      return fn(fd, this.extend(file, fd).offsets);
    } finally {
      fs.closeSync(fd);
    }
  }
  private lines(fd: number, offsets: number[], start: number, end: number) {
    const buf = Buffer.alloc(offsets[end] - offsets[start]);
    fs.readSync(fd, buf, 0, buf.length, offsets[start]);
    return {
      events: buf
        .toString("utf8")
        .split("\n")
        .filter(Boolean)
        .map((l) => JSON.parse(l)),
      bytes: buf.length,
    };
  }
  // Lines written so far: live events carry it so a cached view can later ask for only what follows.
  count(file: string) {
    return this.withFile(file, 0, (_, offsets) => offsets.length - 1);
  }
  // Everything after line `since`, or null when the gap is wider than `limit` (or the log shrank): the caller sends a full page instead.
  after(file: string, since: number, limit: number) {
    return this.withFile(file, null, (fd, offsets) => {
      const total = offsets.length - 1;
      if (since > total || total - since > limit) return null;
      return { ...this.lines(fd, offsets, since, total), start: since, end: total, total };
    });
  }
  read(file: string, before: number | undefined, limit: number) {
    return this.withFile(
      file,
      { events: [] as any[], start: 0, end: 0, total: 0, bytes: 0 },
      (fd, offsets) => {
        const total = offsets.length - 1;
        const end = Math.max(0, Math.min(before ?? total, total));
        const candidate = Math.max(0, end - limit - 150);
        let start = Math.max(0, end - limit);
        let scanned = 0;
        const header = Buffer.alloc(256);
        for (let i = Math.min(start, end - 1); i >= candidate; i--) {
          const size = offsets[i + 1] - offsets[i];
          const n = fs.readSync(fd, header, 0, Math.min(size, header.length), offsets[i]);
          scanned += n;
          // Logs put type first. Inspect that tiny header instead of parsing discarded megabyte tool results.
          let type = header
            .subarray(0, n)
            .toString("utf8")
            .match(/^\s*\{\s*"type"\s*:\s*"([^"]+)"/)?.[1];
          if (!type) {
            const line = Buffer.alloc(size);
            fs.readSync(fd, line, 0, size, offsets[i]);
            scanned += size;
            type = JSON.parse(line.toString("utf8")).type;
          }
          if (type === "user_input") {
            start = i;
            break;
          }
        }
        const { events, bytes } = this.lines(fd, offsets, start, end);
        return { events, start, end, total, bytes: bytes + scanned };
      },
    );
  }
}

    const slowest = [...r.phases].sort((a, b) => b.ms - a.ms)[0]?.name;
    const msg = `slow boot: listening after ${r.totalMs} ms (limit 5000 ms); slowest phase ${slowest}`;
    console.warn(msg); // not an automation step: recording one under an unknown id throws and would crash a slow boot
  },
});

// Bind first and hold requests and upgrades until the state below exists: clients queue instead of being refused.
let serving = false;
let markReady!: () => void;
const ready = new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
  markReady = resolve;
});
const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
  if (serving) return app(req, res);
  void ready.then(() => app(req, res));
});
server.on("upgrade", (req, socket, head) => {
  if (serving) return upgrade(req, socket, head);
  void ready.then(() => upgrade(req, socket, head));
});
await new Promise<void>((resolve) => server.listen(PORT, HOST, resolve));
boot.mark("bound");

const clients = new Set<WebSocket>();
const subscriptions = new WeakMap<WebSocket, string>();
const broadcast = (msg: unknown) => {
  const s = JSON.stringify(msg);
  const event = msg as { t?: string; thread?: string };
  const invalidation =
    event.t === "event" ? JSON.stringify({ t: "invalidate", thread: event.thread }) : null;
  for (const c of clients)
    if (c.readyState === c.OPEN) {
      const selected = subscriptions.get(c);
      c.send(invalidation && selected && selected !== event.thread ? invalidation : s);
    }
};

});

if (process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_MOCK !== "1") {
  const activeRuns = () =>
    threads
      .list()

      .map((t) => ({ provider: t.provider, role: t.issueRun!.role }));
  const dispatcher = createDispatcher({
// ...
    events: automationLog,
    active: () => activeRuns(),
    start: (d) =>
      startIssue(d, {
        create: (o) => threads.create(o),
        send: (id, text) => threads.send(id, text),
      }),
  });
  const reviewer = createReviewer({
    threads: () => threads.list(),
    dataDir: DATA_DIR,
    canReview: (t) => !assignedOwner(t),
    rerouteWorker: (req) => threads.rerouteIssueWorker(req),
    premium: async (req) => {
      const cfg = loadDispatchConfig();
      if (!cfg.enabled || cfg.dryRun || cfg.routing.mode !== "live") return;
      // A persisted assignment also acts as a receipt if the process died before the worker was marked.
      const previous = threads
        .list()
        .find(
          (t) =>
            t.issueRun?.premiumAssignment && canonicalIssue(t.issueRun) === canonicalIssue(req),
        );
      if (previous) return { thread: previous.id };
      const answer = routeWork({
        role: "work",
        effort: "deep",
        premium: true,
        exclude: req.exclude,
// ...
        active: activeRuns(),
        now: Date.now(),
      });
// ...
      return assignPremium({ ...req, ...answer.route, mode: "bypassPermissions" });
    },
    canResume: (t) => {
      const live = threads.get(t.id);
      if (
        !live ||
        live.archived ||
        live.status === "running" ||
        live.background ||
        live.quotaPaused ||
        assignedOwner(live)
      )
        return false;
      const cfg = loadDispatchConfig();
      if (!cfg.enabled || cfg.dryRun || cfg.routing.mode !== "live") return false;
      const answer = routeWork({
        role: "work",
        effort: t.issueRun!.effort as any,
// ...
        active: activeRuns(),
        now: Date.now(),
// ...
      });
      return (
        answer.decision === "route" &&
        [answer.route, ...answer.alternatives.map((a) => a.route)].some(
          (route) => route?.provider === t.provider,
        )
      );
    },
    runner: {
      create: (o) => threads.create(o),
      send: (id, text) => threads.send(id, text),
      mark: (id, patch) => threads.setIssueRun(id, patch),
    },
    route: (effort) =>
      routeWork({
        role: "review",
        effort: effort as any,
// ...
        active: activeRuns(),
        now: Date.now(),
      }),
    events: automationLog,
  });
  threads.onIssueTurnEnd = (t, ev) => {
    if (!assignedOwner(t)) return reviewer.turnEnded(t, ev);
  };
  // Threads stopped by an exhausted premium quota continue late in a later window, when Deep issues would also start.
  const resumeQuotaPaused = () => {
    const cfg = loadDispatchConfig();
    if (!cfg.enabled) return;
// ...
      const waiting = threads
        .list()
        .filter((t) => t.quotaPaused?.provider === provider && !t.archived).length;
      if (!waiting) continue;
      const resumed = cfg.dryRun
        ? 0
        : threads.resumeQuotaPaused(
            provider,
            undefined,
            (t) => !!t.issueRun?.premiumAssignment || !assignedOwner(t),
          );
      automationLog.record({
        step: "dispatcher",
        kind: "ok",
        msg: `${cfg.dryRun ? "dry run: would resume" : "resumed"} ${cfg.dryRun ? waiting : resumed} quota-paused ${provider} thread(s)`,
      });
    }
  };
  const archiver = createAutomationArchiver({ threads });
  let automationSweep: Promise<void> | undefined;
  const sweepAutomation = (archive = false) =>
    (automationSweep ??= (async () => {
      if (archive) await archiver.sweep();
      await reviewer.sweep(threads.list().filter((t) => !assignedOwner(t)));
      // Finished Auto threads keep only transcripts; deleted threads lose their runtime folder.
      const runtime = await sweepAutoRuntime(DATA_DIR, threads.list());
      if (runtime.pruned || runtime.removed || runtime.failed)
        automationLog.record({
          step: "worktree",
          kind: runtime.failed ? "fail" : "info",
          msg: `Auto runtime cleanup: ${runtime.pruned} pruned, ${runtime.removed} removed, ${runtime.failed} failed`,
        });
    })()
      .catch(() => {})
      .finally(() => {
        automationSweep = undefined;
      }));
  void sweepAutomation(true); // catches up on closures missed while the Console was down
// ...
  watchClosed(
// ...
    () => archiver.sweep(),
    () =>
      automationLog.record({
// ...
        kind: "fail",
// ...
      }),
  );
  setInterval(() => {
    void sweepAutomation();
    void dispatcher.tick().catch(() => {});
    resumeQuotaPaused();
  }, 5 * 60_000).unref();
  const opener = createOpener({
// ...
    events: automationLog,
// ...
    claudeAccounts: () =>
      claudeAccounts()
        .filter((a) => a.ready)
        .map((a) => a.id),
    open: (provider, route, account) => {
      const t = threads.create({
        provider,
        ...route,
        ...(account ? { claudeAccount: account } : {}),
        cwd: path.join(os.homedir(), "repos"),
        mode: "bypassPermissions",
// ...
      });
      threads.send(t.id, "Reply with the single word: ok");
      threads.archive(t.id, true);
    },
  });
  setInterval(() => {
    opener.tick().catch(() => {});
  }, 5 * 60_000).unref();
}

// ---- attachments: upload (raw bytes), download, discard. State-changing, so cookie auth alone is not enough: same-origin only.
const sameOrigin: express.RequestHandler = (req, res, next) => {
  try {
    if (new URL(String(req.headers.origin)).host === req.headers.host) return next();
  } catch {}
  res.status(403).json({ error: "Cross-site request refused." });
};
app.post("/login", sameOrigin, express.json({ limit: "1kb" }), (req, res) => {
  if (limited()) return void res.status(429).json({ error: "Too many attempts. Try again later." });
  if (!passwordOk(req.body?.password)) {
    recordFail();
    return void res.status(401).json({ error: "Wrong password." });
  }
  res.setHeader("Set-Cookie", setCookie(makeToken()));
  res.json({ ok: true });
});

const MODEL_RATE_TOKEN_HEADER = "x-agent-console-tool";
app.post("/api/model-request", express.json({ limit: "4kb" }), async (req, res) => {
  const given = Buffer.from(String(req.headers[MODEL_RATE_TOKEN_HEADER] || ""));
  const expected = Buffer.from(String(process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_RATE_TOKEN || ""));
  if (
    !expected.length ||
    given.length !== expected.length ||
    !crypto.timingSafeEqual(given, expected)
  )
    return void res.status(401).json({ error: "Not an Agent Console model request." });
  const provider =
// ...
      ? req.body.provider
      : "other";
  const priority =
    typeof req.body?.priority === "string" && req.body.priority in MODEL_REQUEST_PRIORITIES
      ? req.body.priority
      : "interactive";
  const abort = new AbortController();
  const disconnected = () => abort.abort();
  res.once("close", disconnected);
  try {
    if (req.body?.action === "acquire")
      await acquireModelRequest({ provider, priority, signal: abort.signal });
    else if (req.body?.action === "cooldown")
      await reportModel429(
        typeof req.body.retryAfter === "string" || typeof req.body.retryAfter === "number"
          ? req.body.retryAfter
          : null,
        provider,
      );
    else return void res.status(400).json({ error: "Unknown model admission action." });
    if (!res.destroyed) res.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (error: any) {
    const message =
      error?.code === "MODEL_PROVIDER_QUOTA_EXHAUSTED"
        ? error.message
        : "Model admission unavailable.";
    if (!res.destroyed)
      res.status(503).json({
        error: message,
        message,
        code: error?.code,
        retryAt: error?.retryAt,
        provider: error?.provider,
      });
  } finally {
    res.removeListener("close", disconnected);
  }
});

// ...
  const caller = thread ? threads.get(thread) : undefined;
  const cwd = typeof input.folder === "string" && input.folder ? input.folder : caller?.cwd;
// ...
    version: 1,
    task: input.task,
    cwd: cwd as string,
    caller: { thread, provider: caller?.provider },
  });
// ...
  return researchToolText(res);
}

const cliSearch = new CliSearch();
app.post("/api/agent-tools/:name", express.json({ limit: "1mb" }), async (req, res) => {
  const header = req.headers["x-agent-console-thread"];
  const gate = gateToolCall(
    TOOL_TOKEN,
    String(req.headers["x-agent-console-tool"] || ""),
    typeof header === "string" ? header : undefined,
    req.params.name,
    (threadId) => !!threads.get(threadId)?.issueRun,
  );
  if (gate.status === 401) return void res.status(401).json({ error: gate.error });
  const thread = gate.threadId;
  try {
    if (gate.error) throw new Error(gate.error);
    const input = req.body ?? {};
    const result = ["thread_list", "thread_send"].includes(req.params.name)
      ? await threads.threadTool(req.params.name, thread, input)
// ...
        : req.params.name === "cli_search"
          ? cliSearch.search(input)
          : req.params.name === "session_search"
            ? await sessionSearch.search(input)
            : req.params.name === "session_read"
              ? await sessionSearch.read(input)
// ...
                : isConsoleCliTool(req.params.name)
                  ? await consoleCliTool(req.params.name, input, {
                      ideas,
                      schedules,
                      sessionSearch,
                    })
                  : schedules.tool(req.params.name, thread, input);
    res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-store");
    res.json(result);
  } catch (e: any) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: e?.message || String(e) });
  }
});

app.delete(
  "/api/notifications/subscriptions",
  sameOrigin,
  express.json({ limit: "8kb" }),
  (req, res) => {
    if (!notifications)
      return void res.status(503).json({ error: "Notifications are unavailable." });
    if (typeof req.body?.endpoint !== "string")
      return void res.status(400).json({ error: "Missing subscription endpoint." });
    try {
      notifications.unsubscribe(req.body.endpoint, String(req.headers.origin));
      res.json({ ok: true });
    } catch {
      res.status(503).json({ error: "Could not save notification settings." });
    }

// ...
const turnLatency = (from: number, to: number, thread?: string) => {
  const store = turnTiming();
  if (!store) return { available: false };
  let phone: { provider: string; total_ms: number }[] = [];
  try {
    if (!thread) phone = latency?.samples("first_token", from, to) ?? [];
  } catch {} // device samples are per provider, not per thread
  return { available: true, ...store.summary(from, to, { thread, phone }) };
};
app.get("/api/boot-timing", (_req, res) => {
  res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-store");
  res.json({ boots: boot.recent(20) });
});
app.get("/api/turn-latency", (req, res) => {
  res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-store");
  const to = Number(req.query.to) || Date.now();
  const from = Number(req.query.from) || to - 7 * 86_400_000;
  const thread =
    typeof req.query.thread === "string" && isId(req.query.thread) ? req.query.thread : undefined;
  // ?thread=&detail=N: an existing thread's last N turns (max 20) with their full timelines, for Run details' Full breakdown.
  const known =
    typeof req.query.thread === "string" && threads.get(req.query.thread)
      ? req.query.thread
      : undefined;
  if (req.query.detail != null) {
    const store = turnTiming();
    if (!known) return void res.status(404).json({ error: "Unknown thread." });
    try {
      return void res.json(
        store
          ? { available: true, turns: store.recent(known, Number(req.query.detail) || 10) }
          : { available: false },
      );
    } catch {
      return void res.status(503).json({ available: false });
    }
  }
  if (!(from < to) || to - from > 400 * 86_400_000)
    return void res.status(400).json({ error: "Choose a range of up to 400 days." });
  try {
    res.json(turnLatency(from, to, thread));
  } catch {
    res.status(503).json({ available: false });
  }
});

      process.execPath,
// ...

app.post("/api/automation/assign", sameOrigin, express.json({ limit: "4kb" }), async (req, res) => {
  const { v, repo, number, provider, model, effort, mode, claudeAccount } = req.body ?? {};
  const choice = { v, repo, number, provider, model, effort, mode, claudeAccount };
  // The dialog asks for NDJSON to draw each step live; the last line is the thread or the error.
  if (!String(req.headers.accept).includes("application/x-ndjson")) {
    try {
      return void res.json(await assignPremium(choice));
    } catch (e: any) {
      return void res.status(400).json({ error: String(e?.message ?? e) });
    }
  }
  res.setHeader("Content-Type", "application/x-ndjson");
  res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-store");
  res.setHeader("X-Accel-Buffering", "no");
  res.flushHeaders();
  const line = (msg: unknown) => {
    if (!res.writableEnded)
      res.write(JSON.stringify({ ...(msg as object), at: Date.now() }) + "\n");
  };
  try {
    line({ t: "done", thread: (await assignPremium(choice, line)).thread });
  } catch (e: any) {
    line({ t: "error", error: String(e?.message ?? e) });
  }
  res.end();
});

app.get("/uploads/:thread/:id/:name", (req, res) => {
  const { thread, id, name } = req.params;
  if (!isId(thread) || !isId(id) || !SAFE_NAME.test(name)) return void res.status(404).end();
  const type = sniffImageFile(path.join(UPLOAD_DIR, thread, id, name));
  res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "private, max-age=86400, immutable");
  // only verified images render inline; anything else (html, svg, ...) is a download, so an upload can never run as a page on this origin
  if (type) res.type(type);
  else res.attachment(name).type("application/octet-stream");
  // `root` matters: without it send() 404s any absolute path with a dot segment, and the data dir lives under ~/.config
  res.sendFile(
    `${thread}/${id}/${name}`,
    { root: UPLOAD_DIR },
    (err) => err && !res.headersSent && res.status(404).end(),
  );
});

  if (!req.path.startsWith("/uploads/")) return next(err);

    error:
      err?.type === "entity.too.large"
        ? `Files must be under ${MAX_FILE_BYTES >> 20} MB.`
        : "Upload failed.",
  });
});
// The ```arch route worker runs libavoid's embind glue, which needs eval. Only the worker script (and the module it
// imports) gets a CSP allowing it; Chromium applies a dedicated worker's own response CSP, the page CSP stays strict.
const ARCH_WORKER_SCRIPTS = new Set(["/arch-route-worker.js", "/vendor/libavoid/index.js"]);
app.use((req, res, next) => {
  if (ARCH_WORKER_SCRIPTS.has(req.path))
    res.setHeader(
// ...
      "default-src 'none'; script-src 'self' 'unsafe-eval' 'wasm-unsafe-eval'; connect-src 'self'",
    );
  next();
});

boot.mark("routes");
const wss = new WebSocketServer({
  noServer: true,
  maxPayload: 1 << 20,
  // Browser-supported compression cuts history and tool traffic on remote/mobile links.
  // Independent messages bound memory and avoid retaining prior message dictionaries.
  perMessageDeflate: {
    serverNoContextTakeover: true,
    clientNoContextTakeover: true,
    concurrencyLimit: 2,
    threshold: 1024,
    zlibDeflateOptions: { level: 3 },
  },
});

function upgrade(req: http.IncomingMessage, socket: Duplex, head: Buffer) {
  const okAuth = authed(req);
  // block cross-site websocket hijacking: Origin host must match the Host header
  let okOrigin = false;
  try {
    okOrigin = new URL(String(req.headers.origin)).host === req.headers.host;
  } catch {}
  if (req.url !== "/ws" || !okAuth || !okOrigin) {
    socket.write("HTTP/1.1 403 Forbidden\r\n\r\n");
    return void socket.destroy();
  }
  wss.handleUpgrade(req, socket, head, (ws) => wss.emit("connection", ws));
}

function safeCwd(p: unknown): string {
  if (typeof p !== "string") throw new Error("Invalid directory.");
  const r = path.resolve(p);
  if (r !== HOME && !r.startsWith(HOME + path.sep))
    throw new Error("Directory must be inside your home folder.");
  if (!fs.statSync(r, { throwIfNoEntry: false })?.isDirectory())
    throw new Error("Directory does not exist.");
  return r;
}

function dirs(): string[] {
  const out = [REPOS];
  try {
    for (const e of fs.readdirSync(REPOS, { withFileTypes: true })) {
      if (e.isDirectory() && !e.name.startsWith(".")) out.push(path.join(REPOS, e.name));
    }
  } catch {}
  return out;
}

// Client message ids already handled: a message resent after a reconnect (its ack was lost) is acknowledged, not run twice.
const seenCids = new Map<string, number>();
const firstDelivery = (cid: string) => {
  if (seenCids.has(cid)) return false;
  seenCids.set(cid, Date.now());
  if (seenCids.size > 2000) seenCids.delete(seenCids.keys().next().value!);
  return true;
};

wss.on("connection", (ws) => {
  clients.add(ws);
// ...
  const reply = (m: unknown) => ws.readyState === ws.OPEN && ws.send(JSON.stringify(m));
  reply({
    t: "hello",
    build: BUILD,
    features: {
// ...
      autoIsolation: 1,
      acks: true,
      queueEditing: true,
      questions: true,
      latency: !!latency,
      selectiveEvents: true,
    },
    questions: threads.getQuestions(),
    tasks: schedules.list(),
// ...
    commands: threads.getCommands(),
    threads: threads.list(),
    queues: threads.getQueues(),
    dirs: dirs(),
    providers: providerInfo(),
  });
  let metricsWindow = Date.now(),
    metricsCount = 0;
  // A socket that misses a whole ping interval is dead (phone slept, network changed): drop it so its client reconnects cleanly.
  let isAlive = true;
  ws.on("pong", () => (isAlive = true));
  const ping = setInterval(() => {
    if (!isAlive) return void ws.terminate();
    isAlive = false;
    if (ws.readyState === ws.OPEN) ws.ping();
  }, Number(process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_WS_PING_MS) || 25_000);
  ws.on("close", () => {
    clearInterval(ping);
    clients.delete(ws);
  });
  ws.on("message", (raw) => {
    isAlive = true;
    let m: any;
    try {
      m = JSON.parse(String(raw));
      if (m?.thread != null && !safeId(m.thread)) throw new Error("Invalid thread id.");
      if (m?.t === "read" && m.id != null && !safeId(m.id)) throw new Error("Invalid thread id.");
      switch (m.t) {
        case "ping":
          return reply({ t: "pong" });
// ...
          if (Date.now() - metricsWindow > 60_000) {
            metricsWindow = Date.now();
            metricsCount = 0;
          }
          if (
            !Array.isArray(m.samples) ||
            m.samples.length > 20 ||
            (metricsCount += m.samples.length) > 200
          )
            return;
          try {
            latency?.record(m.samples, (id) => threads.get(id));
          } catch {
            console.warn("Local latency sample write failed.");
          }
          return;
        }
// ...
          return;
        case "answer": {
          try {
            threads.answer(m.thread, m.id, m.answers, m.skip === true);
            return reply({ t: "answer_done", thread: m.thread, id: m.id, request: m.request });
          } catch (e: any) {
            return reply({
              t: "answer_error",
              thread: m.thread,
              id: m.id,
              request: m.request,
              message: e?.message || "Could not send your answer.",
            });
          }
        }
        case "create_issue": {
          const cid = typeof m.cid === "string" ? m.cid : undefined;
          createIssueDraft(threads.get(m.thread)?.cwd, m.draft).then(
            (draft) => reply({ t: "create_issue_done", cid, ...draft }),
            (e: any) =>
              reply({
                t: "create_issue_error",
                cid,
                message: e?.message || "Could not create the issue draft.",
              }),
          );
          return;
        }
        case "queue_edit":
        case "queue_save":
        case "queue_cancel_edit": {
          try {
            if (m.t === "queue_edit")
              return reply({
                t: "queue_edit",
                request: m.request,
                thread: m.thread,
                ...threads.editQueued(m.thread, m.id),
              });
            if (m.t === "queue_save") threads.saveQueued(m.thread, m.id, m.revision, m.text);
            else threads.cancelQueuedEdit(m.thread, m.id, m.revision);
            return reply({ t: "queue_edit_done", request: m.request, thread: m.thread, id: m.id });
          } catch (e: any) {
            return reply({
              t: "queue_edit_error",
              request: m.request,
              message: e?.message || "Could not update this queued message.",
            });
          }
        }
        case "subscribe":
          if (threads.get(m.thread)) subscriptions.set(ws, m.thread);
          return;
        case "open":
          if (m.foregroundOnly === true && threads.get(m.thread)) subscriptions.set(ws, m.thread);
          return reply({
            t: "history",
            thread: m.thread,
            request: m.request,
            before: Number.isInteger(m.before) ? m.before : null,
            ...threads.page(
              m.thread,
              Number.isInteger(m.before) ? m.before : undefined,
              Number.isInteger(m.since) && m.since >= 0 ? m.since : undefined,
              m.small === true,
            ),
          });
        case "create": {
          const t = threads.create({
            provider: m.provider,
            claudeAccount: typeof m.claudeAccount === "string" ? m.claudeAccount : undefined,
            cwd: safeCwd(m.cwd),
            mode: m.mode,
            model: m.model,
            effort: m.effort,
            title: m.title,
          });
          return reply({ t: "created", thread: t });
        }
        case "send":
          if (typeof m.cid === "string" && !firstDelivery(m.cid))
            return reply({ t: "ack", cid: m.cid });
          if (
            typeof m.text === "string" &&
            (m.text.trim() || (Array.isArray(m.attachments) && m.attachments.length))
          ) {
            threads.send(m.thread, m.text.slice(0, 100_000), {
              attachments: m.attachments,
              mode: m.mode === "steer" ? "steer" : "queue",
            });
          }
          return typeof m.cid === "string" && reply({ t: "ack", cid: m.cid });
        case "steer_queued":
          return threads.steerQueued(m.thread, m.id);
        case "compact":
          return threads.compact(m.thread);
        case "unqueue":
          return threads.unqueue(m.thread, String(m.id));
        case "fork":
          return reply({
            t: "created",
            thread: threads.fork(
              m.thread,
              typeof m.provider === "string" ? m.provider : undefined,
              m.context === "recent",
              {
                ...Object.fromEntries(
                  ["model", "effort", "mode", "claudeAccount"]
                    .filter((k) => typeof m[k] === "string")
                    .map((k) => [k, m[k]]),
                ),
              },
            ),
          });
        case "abort":
          if (typeof m.cid === "string" && !firstDelivery(m.cid))
            return reply({ t: "ack", cid: m.cid });
          threads.abort(m.thread);
          return typeof m.cid === "string" && reply({ t: "ack", cid: m.cid });
        case "warm":
          if (typeof m.thread === "string") threads.warm(m.thread);
          return;
        case "read":
          if (typeof m.id === "string") threads.markRead(m.id);
          return;
        case "update": {
          return threads.update(m.thread, {
            mode: typeof m.mode === "string" ? m.mode : undefined,
            model: typeof m.model === "string" ? m.model : undefined,
            effort: typeof m.effort === "string" ? m.effort : undefined,
            claudeAccount: typeof m.claudeAccount === "string" ? m.claudeAccount : undefined,
            advisor: typeof m.advisor === "string" ? m.advisor : undefined,
// ...
            title: typeof m.title === "string" ? m.title : undefined,
          });
        }
        case "task_save": {
          const input = {
            title: m.title,
            prompt: m.prompt,
            provider: m.provider,
            model: m.model,
            effort: m.effort,
            mode: m.mode,
            cwd: m.cwd === undefined ? undefined : safeCwd(m.cwd),
            repeat: m.repeat,
            time: m.time,
            day: m.day,
            tz: m.tz,
            enabled: typeof m.enabled === "boolean" ? m.enabled : undefined,
            saveOutput: typeof m.saveOutput === "boolean" ? m.saveOutput : undefined,
          };
          const task =
            typeof m.id === "string" ? schedules.update(m.id, input) : schedules.create(input);
          return reply({ t: "task_saved", request: m.request, task });
        }
        case "task_delete":
          return schedules.remove(String(m.id));
        case "task_run": {
          const run = schedules.run(String(m.id), "manual");
          if (run.status === "skipped") return reply({ t: "error", message: run.reason });
          if (run.status === "failed") return reply({ t: "error", message: run.reason });
          return run.thread && reply({ t: "created", thread: threads.get(run.thread) });
        }
        case "archive":
          return threads.archive(m.thread, m.archived !== false);
        case "delete":
          return threads.remove(m.thread);
      }
    } catch (e: any) {
      reply({
        t: "error",
        message: e?.message ?? String(e),
        ...(typeof m?.cid === "string" ? { cid: m.cid } : {}),
      });
    }
  });
});

  bridgeServer.on("error", () =>
    console.error(

// Runs once the state and routes exist; the port is already bound, so this is the old listen callback.
function startServing() {
  const defaultDataDir = path.join(os.homedir(), ".config", "agent-console");
// ...
    pidFile: PID_FILE || (DATA_DIR === defaultDataDir ? path.join(RUN_DIR, "pid") : undefined),
  });
  if (PID_FILE) fs.writeFileSync(PID_FILE, String(process.pid)); // only the process that holds the port may claim it
  serving = true;
  markReady(); // queued requests and upgrades run once this synchronous block yields
  startModelRateBroker(PORT); // same rule: a scratch server that lost the port must not repoint the live broker
  startHostSampler(); // 5 s host samples for the Host page's history charts
  workspaceMonitor?.start(); // reads those samples; alerts and escalation (docs/monitoring.md)
  boot.mark("listening");
  boot.flush("listen");
  console.log(`agent-console listening on http://${HOST}:${PORT}`);
  console.log(
    CODER_HOSTS.length
      ? `auth: Coder SSO for ${CODER_HOSTS.join(", ")} (password cookie still works elsewhere); proxy proof ${PROXY_PROOF_ON ? "on" : "OFF (SSO disabled, fail closed)"}`
      : "auth: password only",
  );
  if (process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_SHOW_PASSWORD) console.log(`password: ${PASSWORD}`);
  // Connected clients may have received the previous process's cached model list.
// ...
    .then(() => broadcast({ t: "providers", providers: providerInfo() }))
// ...
  // Boot work nothing waits on runs once the first page has had its turn on the event loop.
  setTimeout(() => {
// ...
    // A proxy the previous server left exiting must be gone first, or it would look like an external one.
// ...
      .reapOrphan(ORPHAN_FILE)
// ...
    threads.warmAccounting();
    boot.mark("accounting-warm");
    void sessionSearch
      .warm()
      .catch(() => console.warn("Session search index will retry on the next query."));
    boot.flush("deferred");
  }, 1000).unref();
}

// A hash of the code this process loaded. Open tabs compare it on reconnect and reload themselves when the server was replaced
// (`bin/agent-console restart-when-idle` -> scripts/idle-restart.mjs).
const sources = (dir: string): string[] =>
  fs
    .readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })
    .flatMap((e) =>
// ...
        ? []
        : e.isDirectory()
          ? sources(path.join(dir, e.name))
          : [path.join(dir, e.name)],
    );
// The release sha from RELEASE.json, when a release wrote one. Missing or unreadable file: null.
function releaseSha(): string | null {
  try {
    const sha = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, "RELEASE.json"), "utf8"))?.sha;
    return typeof sha === "string" && sha.length > 0 ? sha : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}
function buildId() {
  const sha = releaseSha();
  if (sha) return sha.slice(0, 12);
  const h = crypto.createHash("sha1");
  for (const f of [
    ...sources(path.join(ROOT, "server")),
    ...sources(path.join(ROOT, "public")),
  ].sort()) {
    const st = fs.statSync(f);
    h.update(`${f}:${st.mtimeMs}:${st.size}`);
  }
  return h.digest("hex").slice(0, 12);
}
const BUILD = buildId();

// Standby (#348): announce readiness with our pid, then block until the supervisor writes `go` (stdin EOF first: give up).
function waitForGo(runDir: string): Promise<void> {
  fs.mkdirSync(runDir, { recursive: true });
  const readyFile = path.join(runDir, "standby-ready");
  fs.writeFileSync(`${readyFile}.tmp`, String(process.pid));
  fs.renameSync(`${readyFile}.tmp`, readyFile); // the supervisor never reads a half-written pid
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    let pending = "";
    let started = false;
    const onData = (chunk: string) => {
      pending += chunk;
      const lines = pending.split("\n");
      pending = lines.pop() ?? "";
      if (started || !lines.some((line) => line.trim() === "go")) return;
      started = true;
      process.stdin.off("data", onData).pause();
      resolve();
    };
    const onEnd = () => {
      if (started) return;
      console.error("standby: stdin closed before go; exiting");
      process.exit(1);
    };
    process.stdin.setEncoding("utf8").on("data", onData).on("end", onEnd).on("error", onEnd);
  });
}

// ...
const RESTART_CLOSE_CODE = 1012; // WebSocket "service restart": tabs reconnect to the replacement server
const settled = (work: Promise<unknown>, ms: number) =>
  Promise.race([work.catch(() => {}), new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms))]);

// Node cannot tell who sent the signal; the sender is usually among the newest processes, so log those
function newestProcesses(): string {
  try {
    return execFileSync("ps", ["-eo", "pid,ppid,etime,args", "--sort=-start_time"], {
      encoding: "utf8",
    })
      .split("\n")
      .slice(0, 16)
      .map((line) => line.slice(0, 240))
      .join("\n");
  } catch {
    return "";
  }
}

// Stop accepting first, then tell every tab to reconnect; resolves when the tabs answered the close.
function closeClients(): Promise<unknown> {
  server.close();
  const closed = [...clients].map(
    (socket) =>
      new Promise((resolve) => {
        socket.once("close", resolve);
        socket.close(RESTART_CLOSE_CODE, "server restart");
      }),
  );
  server.closeAllConnections();
  return Promise.all(closed);
}

// ...
async function shutdown(sig: string) {
  const answered = closeClients();
  const handoff = fs.existsSync(path.join(RUN_DIR, "handoff"));
  const newest = handoff ? "(restart handoff)" : newestProcesses();
  console.log(`[${new Date().toISOString()}] stopping on ${sig}; newest processes:\n${newest}`);
  schedules.shutdown();
  threads.shutdown();
// ...
  benchmarks.close();
  workspaceMonitor?.stop();
// ...
  latency?.close();
  process.exit(0);
}

let shuttingDown = false;
for (const sig of ["SIGINT", "SIGTERM"] as const) {
  process.on(sig, () => {
    if (shuttingDown) return;
    shuttingDown = true;
    void shutdown(sig);
  });
}
startServing();

File: server/latency.ts

// Filtered excerpt: some source lines are omitted.
import fs from "node:fs";
import { DatabaseSync } from "node:sqlite";

// first_token: send tap -> first reply token painted on this device (wait_ms = attachment uploads still in flight at the tap)
const KINDS = new Set([
  "open",
  "page",
  "cached",
  "refresh",
  "cancelled",
  "viewer_open",
  "viewer_tree",
  "viewer_module",
  "first_token",
]);
const STAGES = [
  "server_ms",
  "render_ms",
  "wait_ms",
  "prepare_ms",
  "roundtrip_ms",
  "decode_ms",
  "module_ms",
];
const duration = (n: unknown) =>
  typeof n === "number" && Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 0 && n <= 300_000;
const size = (n: unknown, max: number) =>
  typeof n === "number" && Number.isInteger(n) && n >= 0 && n <= max;

function validLatencyShape(sample: any) {
  if (!size(sample.width, 16384) || !sample.width || !size(sample.height, 16384) || !sample.height)
    return false;
  if (STAGES.some((stage) => sample[stage] != null && !duration(sample[stage]))) return false;
  if (sample.events != null && !size(sample.events, 100_000)) return false;
  return !["bytes", "wire_bytes"].some(
    (field) => sample[field] != null && !size(sample[field], 64 * 1024 * 1024),
  );
}

function latencyRow(
  sample: any,
  thread: (id: string) => { provider: string } | undefined,
  now: number,
): any[] | undefined {
  if (
    !sample ||
    !KINDS.has(sample.kind) ||
    typeof sample.thread !== "string" ||
    !duration(sample.total_ms)
  )
    return undefined;
  const owner =
    sample.thread === "viewer:repos" && sample.kind.startsWith("viewer_")
      ? { provider: "workspace" }
      : thread(sample.thread);
  if (!owner || !validLatencyShape(sample)) return undefined;
  return [
    now,
    sample.thread,
    owner.provider,
    sample.kind,
    sample.total_ms,
    sample.server_ms ?? null,
    sample.render_ms ?? null,
    sample.wait_ms ?? null,
    sample.events ?? null,
    sample.bytes ?? null,
    sample.wire_bytes ?? null,
    sample.prepare_ms ?? null,
    sample.roundtrip_ms ?? null,
    sample.decode_ms ?? null,
    sample.module_ms ?? null,
    sample.width,
    sample.height,
    sample.touch === true ? 1 : 0,
    sample.hidden === true ? 1 : 0,
  ];
}

export const RETENTION_DAYS = 7;
const DAY_MS = 86_400_000;

// Local, bounded measurements only. Never accept arbitrary metadata, URLs, prompts or browser fingerprints.
export class LatencyStore {
  private db: DatabaseSync;
  private writes = 0;
  private maxSamples: number;
  constructor(file: string, maxSamples = 20_000) {
    this.maxSamples = maxSamples;
    fs.closeSync(fs.openSync(file, "a", 0o600));
    fs.chmodSync(file, 0o600);
    this.db = new DatabaseSync(file);
    this.db.exec(`PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL; CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS latency (
      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, at INTEGER NOT NULL, thread TEXT NOT NULL, provider TEXT NOT NULL,
      kind TEXT NOT NULL, total_ms REAL NOT NULL, server_ms REAL, render_ms REAL, wait_ms REAL,
      events INTEGER, bytes INTEGER, wire_bytes INTEGER, prepare_ms REAL, roundtrip_ms REAL, decode_ms REAL, module_ms REAL, width INTEGER NOT NULL, height INTEGER NOT NULL,
      touch INTEGER NOT NULL, hidden INTEGER NOT NULL
    ); CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS latency_at ON latency(at);`);
    const columns = new Set(
      this.db
        .prepare("PRAGMA table_info(latency)")
        .all()
        .map((c: any) => c.name),
    );
    for (const name of ["wire_bytes", "prepare_ms", "roundtrip_ms", "decode_ms", "module_ms"])
      if (!columns.has(name)) this.db.exec(`ALTER TABLE latency ADD COLUMN ${name} REAL`);
    this.prune();
  }
  record(
    samples: unknown,
    thread: (id: string) => { provider: string } | undefined,
    now = Date.now(),
  ) {
    if (!Array.isArray(samples) || samples.length > 20) return 0;
    const rows: any[][] = [];
    for (const sample of samples) {
      const row = latencyRow(sample, thread, now);
      if (row) rows.push(row);
    }
    if (!rows.length) return 0;
    const insert = this.db.prepare(
      "INSERT INTO latency(at,thread,provider,kind,total_ms,server_ms,render_ms,wait_ms,events,bytes,wire_bytes,prepare_ms,roundtrip_ms,decode_ms,module_ms,width,height,touch,hidden) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
    );
    this.db.exec("BEGIN");
    try {
      for (const row of rows) insert.run(...row);
      this.db.exec("COMMIT");
    } catch (error) {
      this.db.exec("ROLLBACK");
      throw error;
    }
    this.writes += rows.length;
    if (this.writes >= 100) {
      this.writes = 0;
      this.prune(now);
    }
    return rows.length;
  }
  private prune(now = Date.now()) {
    this.db.prepare("DELETE FROM latency WHERE at < ?").run(now - RETENTION_DAYS * DAY_MS);
    this.db
      .prepare(
        "DELETE FROM latency WHERE id <= (SELECT id FROM latency ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1 OFFSET ?)",
      )
      .run(this.maxSamples);
  }
  summary(now = Date.now()) {
    this.prune(now);
    const rows = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM latency ORDER BY total_ms").all() as any[];
    const groups = new Map<string, any[]>();
    const add = (key: string, row: any) => {
      const group = groups.get(key) || [];
      group.push(row);
      groups.set(key, group);
    };
    for (const row of rows) {
      add(row.kind, row);
      if (!row.hidden) add(`${row.kind}/visible`, row);
      add(
        `${row.kind}/${row.provider}/${row.width <= 600 ? "mobile" : row.width <= 1024 ? "tablet" : "desktop"}/${row.hidden ? "background" : "visible"}`,
        row,
      );
    }
    // latest 20 foreground samples per kind: shows a fix (or a regression) that the retention-window pool hides
    for (const [key, group] of [...groups].filter(
      ([key]) => key.endsWith("/visible") && key.split("/").length === 2,
    ))
      groups.set(
        `${key}/recent`,
        [...group]
          .sort((a, b) => b.at - a.at || b.id - a.id)
          .slice(0, 20)
          .sort((a, b) => a.total_ms - b.total_ms),
      );
    const stats = (group: any[]) => ({
      count: group.length,
      median_ms: group[Math.floor((group.length - 1) * 0.5)].total_ms,
      p95_ms: group[Math.ceil(group.length * 0.95) - 1].total_ms,
      max_ms: group.at(-1).total_ms,
      mean_ms: Math.round(group.reduce((n, r) => n + r.total_ms, 0) / group.length),
    });
// ...
    // Missing stages stay absent, including on databases created before viewer measurements.
    const viewerStats = (group: any[]) => ({
      stages: Object.fromEntries(
        STAGES.flatMap((key) => {
          const values = group
            .filter((row) => row[key] != null)
            .map((row) => ({ total_ms: row[key] }))
            .sort((a, b) => a.total_ms - b.total_ms);
          return values.length ? [[key, stats(values)]] : [];
        }),
      ),
      counts: Object.fromEntries(
        ["events", "bytes", "wire_bytes"].flatMap((key) => {
          const values = group.filter((row) => row[key] != null).map((row) => row[key]);
          return values.length
            ? [
                [
                  key,
                  {
                    count: values.length,
                    total: values.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0),
                    max: Math.max(...values),
                  },
                ],
              ]
            : [];
        }),
      ),
    });
    // The Run details table picks its own percentiles, so its two windows carry every sorted time (whole ms keep it small).
    const shown = (key: string) => /^[^/]+\/visible(\/recent)?$/.test(key);
    return {
      retention_days: RETENTION_DAYS,
      max_samples: this.maxSamples,
      count: rows.length,
      groups: Object.fromEntries(
        [...groups].map(([key, values]) => [
          key,
          {
            ...stats(values),
            ...(key.startsWith("viewer_") ? viewerStats(values) : {}),
            ...(shown(key) ? { samples_ms: values.map((r) => Math.round(r.total_ms)) } : {}),
          },
        ]),
      ),
      slowest: rows.slice(-10).reverse(),
      recent: this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM latency ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 20").all(),
    };
  }
// ...
  samples(kind: string, from: number, to: number) {
    return this.db
      .prepare(
        "SELECT provider, total_ms FROM latency WHERE kind = ? AND hidden = 0 AND at BETWEEN ? AND ? ORDER BY total_ms",
      )
      .all(kind, from, to) as { provider: string; total_ms: number }[];
  }
  close() {
    this.db.close();
  }
}


================
File: server/log-stamp.ts

import { StringDecoder } from "node:string_decoder";

type Write = (chunk: any, encoding?: any, callback?: any) => boolean;
type Stream = { write: Write };

// ISO 8601 with milliseconds and the local UTC offset, e.g. 2026-10-25T15:24:46.123+00:00 (same shape as bin/agent-console).
export function isoNow(date = new Date()): string {
  const pad = (value: number, width = 2) => String(value).padStart(width, "0");
  const offset = -date.getTimezoneOffset();
  const sign = offset < 0 ? "-" : "+";
  const abs = Math.abs(offset);
  return (
    `${date.getFullYear()}-${pad(date.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(date.getDate())}` +
    `T${pad(date.getHours())}:${pad(date.getMinutes())}:${pad(date.getSeconds())}.${pad(date.getMilliseconds(), 3)}` +
    `${sign}${pad(Math.floor(abs / 60))}:${pad(abs % 60)}`
  );
}

// Wraps stream.write so every output line starts with a timestamp; the rest of each line is unchanged.
// Returns a function that restores the original write.
export function stampLines(stream: Stream, now: () => string = isoNow): () => void {
  const original = stream.write;
  const decoder = new StringDecoder("utf8");
  let atLineStart = true;
  stream.write = function (this: unknown, chunk: any, encoding?: any, callback?: any) {
    if (typeof chunk !== "string" && !(chunk instanceof Uint8Array))
      return original.call(this, chunk, encoding, callback);
    const text = typeof chunk === "string" ? chunk : decoder.write(Buffer.from(chunk));
    let out = "";
    for (const part of text.split(/(?<=\n)/)) {
      if (atLineStart) out += `${now()} `;
      out += part;
      atLineStart = part.endsWith("\n");
    }
    const writeDone = typeof encoding === "function" ? encoding : callback;
    return original.call(this, out, "utf8", writeDone);
  } as Write;
  return () => {
    stream.write = original;
  };
}

File: server/questions.ts

import {
  type Agent,
  cleanQuestions,
  type Question,
  type QuestionReply,
} from "./providers/types.ts";

export interface PendingQuestion {
  type: "question";
  id: string;
  questions: Question[];
  ts?: number;
  async?: true;
}

function validateAnswerValues(question: Question, values: unknown): string[] {
  if (
    !Array.isArray(values) ||
    !values.length ||
    values.length > 20 ||
    values.some((value) => typeof value !== "string" || !value.trim() || value.length > 10_000)
  )
    throw new Error("Answer each question before continuing.");
  if (!question.multiSelect && values.length !== 1)
    throw new Error("Choose one answer for this question.");
  if (new Set(values).size !== values.length) throw new Error("Duplicate answer.");
  if (
    question.options.length &&
    !question.allowOther &&
    values.some((value) => !question.options.some((option) => option.label === value))
  )
    throw new Error("Choose one of the available answers.");
  return values;
}

function collectAnswers(
  request: PendingQuestion,
  raw: Record<string, unknown>,
  answers: Record<string, string[]>,
) {
  for (const key of Object.keys(raw))
    if (!request.questions.some((question) => question.id === key))
      throw new Error("Unknown question.");
  for (const question of request.questions)
    answers[question.id] = validateAnswerValues(question, raw[question.id]);
}

function buildAnswerEvent(
  request: PendingQuestion,
  requestId: string,
  answers: Record<string, string[]>,
  skip: boolean,
  note: string | undefined,
) {
  const visible = Object.fromEntries(
    request.questions
      .filter((question) => answers[question.id])
      .map((question) => [question.id, question.secret ? ["••••••"] : answers[question.id]]),
  );
  return {
    type: "question_answer",
    id: requestId,
    answers: visible,
    ...(skip ? { skip: true } : {}),
    ...(note !== undefined
      ? { note: request.questions.some((question) => question.secret) ? "••••••" : note }
      : {}),
    ts: Date.now(),
  };
}

// Blocking requests belong to a live process; async messages remain answerable after it exits.
export class Questions {
  private threads = new Map<string, Map<string, PendingQuestion>>();
  list(thread: string): PendingQuestion[] {
    return [...(this.threads.get(thread)?.values() ?? [])];
  }
  all() {
    return Object.fromEntries([...this.threads].map(([id]) => [id, this.list(id)]));
  }
  add(thread: string, event: any): PendingQuestion {
    if (typeof event.id !== "string" || !event.id || event.id.length > 500)
      throw new Error("Invalid question request.");
    const questions = cleanQuestions(event.questions);
    if (!questions.length || new Set(questions.map((q) => q.id)).size !== questions.length)
      throw new Error("Invalid question request.");
    if (event.async && questions.some((q) => q.secret))
      throw new Error("Secret fields require a blocking question.");
    const pending = this.threads.get(thread) ?? new Map();
    if (pending.has(event.id)) throw new Error("Duplicate question request.");
    const request = { ...event, type: "question" as const, questions };
    pending.set(event.id, request);
    this.threads.set(thread, pending);
    return request;
  }
  remove(thread: string, id: string) {
    const pending = this.threads.get(thread);
    const request = pending?.get(id);
    pending?.delete(id);
    if (!pending?.size) this.threads.delete(thread);
    return request;
  }
  answer(
    thread: string,
    id: string,
    raw: unknown,
    agent?: Agent,
    skip = false,
    note?: string,
    deliver?: (reply: QuestionReply) => boolean,
  ) {
    const request = this.threads.get(thread)?.get(id);
    if (!request || (!deliver && (!agent?.alive || !agent.answer)))
      throw new Error("This question is no longer waiting for an answer.");
    if (!skip && note === undefined && (!raw || typeof raw !== "object" || Array.isArray(raw)))
      throw new Error("Invalid answers.");
    const answers: Record<string, string[]> = Object.create(null);
    if (!skip && note === undefined)
      collectAnswers(request, raw as Record<string, unknown>, answers);
    const reply: QuestionReply = {
      answers,
      ...(skip ? { skip: true } : {}),
      ...(note !== undefined ? { note } : {}),
    };
    // Remove first: an adapter may synchronously emit completion or a result while writing the response.
    this.remove(thread, id);
    let consumed: boolean;
    try {
      consumed = deliver ? deliver(reply) : agent!.answer!(id, reply);
    } catch (error) {
      this.add(thread, request);
      throw error;
    }
    return {
      consumed,
      event: buildAnswerEvent(request, id, answers, skip, note),
    };
  }
}
