      background: [...(live?.backgroundTools ?? [])],
      backgroundCount: live
        ? Math.max(this.threads.get(id)?.background ?? 0, live.backgroundTools?.size ?? 0)
        : 0,
    };
  }

  private emitWorkState(thread: Thread) {
    thread.workState = this.workState(thread.id);
    this.emit({ t: "thread", thread });
  }

  private noteWorkState(thread: Thread, event: any) {
    if (event.parent_tool_use_id) return;
    const live = this.live.get(thread.id);
    if (!live) return;
    const work = {
      thread,
      event,
      foreground: (live.foreground ??= new Set<string>()),
      background: (live.backgroundTools ??= new Set<string>()),
      tasks: (live.taskTools ??= new Map<string, string>()),
    };
    let changed = false;
    switch (event.type) {
      case "assistant":
        changed = this.trackWorkCalls(work);
        break;
      case "user":
        changed = this.trackWorkResults(work);
        break;
      case "system":
        changed = this.trackSystemWork(work);
        break;
    }
    if (changed) this.emitWorkState(thread);
  }

  private trackSystemWork(work: WorkUpdate) {
    switch (work.event.subtype) {
      case "task_started":
        return this.startTaskWork(work);
      case "task_notification":
        return this.finishTaskWork(work);
      case "background_tasks_changed":
        if (Array.isArray(work.event.tasks)) this.replaceBackgroundWork(work);
        return false;
      default:
        return false;
    }
  }

  private trackWorkCalls({ event, foreground }: WorkUpdate) {
    let changed = false;
    for (const block of event.message?.content ?? []) {
      if (block.type !== "tool_use" || typeof block.id !== "string") continue;
      foreground.add(block.id);
      changed = true;
    }
    return changed;
  }

  private trackWorkResults({ event, foreground, background, tasks }: WorkUpdate) {
    let changed = false;
    for (const block of event.message?.content ?? []) {
      if (block.type !== "tool_result") continue;
      changed = foreground.delete(block.tool_use_id) || changed;
      if (background.has(block.tool_use_id)) continue;
      for (const [task, tool] of tasks) if (tool === block.tool_use_id) tasks.delete(task);
    }
    return changed;
  }

  private startTaskWork({ event, foreground, background, tasks }: WorkUpdate) {
    if (typeof event.tool_use_id !== "string") return false;
    if (typeof event.task_id === "string") tasks.set(event.task_id, event.tool_use_id);
    if (!event.is_backgrounded) return false;
    foreground.delete(event.tool_use_id);
    background.add(event.tool_use_id);
    return true;
  }

  private finishTaskWork({ thread, event, background, tasks }: WorkUpdate) {
    const id = event.tool_use_id ?? tasks.get(event.task_id);
    tasks.delete(event.task_id);
    if (id) for (const [task, tool] of tasks) if (tool === id) tasks.delete(task);
    if (!id || !background.delete(id)) return false;
    thread.background = Math.max(0, (thread.background ?? 0) - 1);
    return true;
  }

  private replaceBackgroundWork({ event, foreground, background, tasks }: WorkUpdate) {
    background.clear();
    for (const task of event.tasks) {
      const id = task.tool_use_id ?? tasks.get(task.task_id);
      if (typeof id === "string") background.add(id);
    }
    for (const [task, tool] of tasks)
      if (!foreground.has(tool) && !background.has(tool)) tasks.delete(task);
  }

// ...
  private noteBackgroundTasks(thread: Thread, event: any): boolean {
    if (
      event.type !== "system" ||
      event.subtype !== "background_tasks_changed" ||
      !Array.isArray(event.tasks)
    )
      return false;
    const count = event.tasks.length;
    if ((thread.background ?? 0) !== count) {
      if (count) thread.background = count;
      else delete thread.background;
      this.emit({ t: "thread", thread });
// ...
    }
    this.emitWorkState(thread);
    return true;
  }

// ...
  private noteConsoleError(thread: Thread, event: any) {
    if (event.type !== "console_error" || typeof event.text !== "string") return;
    const turn = this.turns.get(thread.id);
    if (!turn) return;
    turn.failure = event.text;
    turn.failureCode = typeof event.code === "string" ? event.code : undefined;
  }

  // Claude starts a turn by itself when a background subagent finishes after the turn ended: show it as running
  private noteSelfStartedTurn(thread: Thread, event: any, compacting: boolean) {
    if (event.type !== "system" || event.subtype !== "init") return;
    if (thread.status !== "idle" || thread.provider !== "claude" || compacting) return;
    thread.status = "running";
    this.turns.set(thread.id, { start: Date.now(), tools: {} });
    this.touch(thread);
  }

  // The turn clock times the event and learns the model the process resolved; Codex names it in its init event
  private noteResolvedModel(thread: Thread, event: any) {
    const clock = this.turns.get(thread.id)?.clock;
    if (clock)
      timed(() => {
        clock.event(event);
        const live = this.live.get(thread.id);
        if (live && clock.resolvedModel) live.model = clock.resolvedModel;
      });
    if (
      thread.provider === "codex" &&
      event.type === "system" &&
      event.subtype === "init" &&
      typeof event.model === "string"
    ) {
      thread.resolvedModel = event.model;
    }
  }

  // A question becomes pending (the stored copy replaces the event) and an answer clears it. True: invalid or unknown, so dropped.
  private noteQuestion(thread: Thread, flow: EventFlow): boolean {
    if (flow.event.type === "question") {
      try {
        flow.event = this.questions.add(thread.id, flow.event);
      } catch {
        this.publish(thread.id, {
          type: "console_error",
          text: "The agent sent an invalid question request.",
        });
        return true;
      }
      this.updateWaiting(thread.id);
      if (!flow.compacting && this.notificationTurns.has(thread.id))
        this.notify(thread, "needs_input");
    }
    if (flow.event.type === "question_answer") {
      if (!this.questions.remove(thread.id, flow.event.id)) return true;
      this.updateWaiting(thread.id);
    }
    return false;
  }

  // Codex quota windows. Not part of the conversation.
// ...
    const windows = codexWindows(event.rateLimits);
    if (windows.length) {
      const merged = new Map(
// ...
      );
      for (const window of windows) merged.set(window.label, window); // app-server quota notifications can be sparse
// ...
    }
    return true;
  }

  // Claude quota windows, stamped with the account they belong to and when they were seen
  private noteRateLimit(thread: Thread, flow: EventFlow) {
    if (flow.event.type !== "rate_limit_event") return;
    const windows = claudeWindows(flow.event);
    if (!windows.length) return;
    const provider =
// ...
    if (!provider.startsWith("claude")) {
// ...
      return;
    }
    const source = flow.event;
    const observedAt =
// ...
        ? source.observedAt
        : Date.now();
// ...
    flow.event = event;
// ...
      observedAt: event.observedAt,
// ...
      ...(typeof event.session_id === "string" && typeof event.uuid === "string"
        ? { sourceSessionId: event.session_id, sourceEventUuid: event.uuid }
        : {}),
    });
  }

// ...
  private noteRunSettings(thread: Thread, event: any): boolean {
// ...
      const turn = this.turns.get(thread.id);
// ...
    }
// ...
    thread.lastRun = {
      ...(event.type === "run_settings" ? {} : thread.lastRun),
      ...Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(event).filter(([key]) => key !== "type")),
    };
    this.touch(thread);
    return true;
  }

// ...
    // not part of the conversation: remember it on the thread so the context meter survives reloads
    thread.context = {
      used: event.reset ? undefined : (event.used ?? thread.context?.used),
      max: event.max ?? thread.context?.max,
      ...(event.source ? { source: event.source } : {}),
    };
    this.emit({ t: "thread", thread });
    return true;
  }

  private noteSession(thread: Thread, event: any) {
    if (
      event.type !== "system" ||
      event.subtype !== "init" ||
      !event.session_id ||
      thread.sessionId === event.session_id
    )
      return;
    if (thread.forkFrom) this.accounting.seedFork(event.session_id, thread.forkFrom);
    thread.sessionId = event.session_id;
    delete thread.forkFrom; // the fork now has its own session
    this.persist();
  }

  private noteCompactBoundary(thread: Thread, event: any) {
    if (event.type !== "system" || event.subtype !== "compact_boundary") return;
    const live = this.live.get(thread.id);
    if (live) live.compactConfirmed = true;
    thread.lastCompactedAt = Date.now();
// ...
    thread.context = { max: thread.context?.max, source: thread.context?.source };
    this.emit({ t: "thread", thread });
  }

// ...
  private noteTurnContent(thread: Thread, event: any) {
    const turn = this.turns.get(thread.id);
    if (event.type === "assistant" && turn) {
      for (const block of event.message?.content ?? [])
        if (block?.type === "tool_use") turn.tools[block.name] = (turn.tools[block.name] ?? 0) + 1;
      this.trackBlocks(turn, event.message?.content ?? []);
    }
    if (event.type === "user") this.trackResults(turn, event.message?.content);
  }

  // The turn ended. True while Claude's empty /compact acknowledgement waits for the real result (nothing is logged yet).
  private noteResult(thread: Thread, flow: EventFlow): boolean {
    const questionOpen = this.questions.list(thread.id).length > 0; // cancelled just below; the turn still ended on it
    this.cancelQuestions(thread.id);
    if (this.awaitCompaction(thread, flow.event)) return true;
    const live = this.live.get(thread.id);
    live?.foreground?.clear();
    if (live?.taskTools)
      for (const [task, tool] of live.taskTools)
        if (!live.backgroundTools?.has(tool)) live.taskTools.delete(task);
    this.emitWorkState(thread);
    const failedTurn = this.turns.get(thread.id);
    const failure = failedTurn?.failure;
    const unfinished = questionOpen || !!failedTurn?.bgTools?.size;
    if (flow.event.is_error) flow.issueError = resultError(flow.event, failure);
    this.finishTurn(thread, flow.event);
// ...
    this.noteQuotaPause(thread, flow.event, failure);
    if (!flow.compacting) {
      thread.replyAt = Date.now();
      thread.lastProviderActivityAt = Math.max(Date.now(), thread.lastUserActivityAt ?? 0);
// ...
    }
    if (thread.operation === "compact") this.finishCompaction(thread, flow);
    thread.lastRun = { ...thread.lastRun, duration_ms: flow.event.duration_ms };
    // a message that arrived just as the turn ended starts the next one straight away: Claude says so in queued_turn_count
    thread.status = Number(flow.event.queued_turn_count) > 0 ? "running" : "idle";
    this.notifyTurnEnd(thread, flow);
    this.touch(thread);
    this.recycleRouting(thread.id);
// ...
    this.continueAfterResult(thread, flow.event, unfinished);
    return false;
  }

  // Claude answers /compact with an empty result straight away, then compacts and sends compact_boundary and the real result
  private awaitCompaction(thread: Thread, event: any): boolean {
    const live = this.live.get(thread.id);
    if (
      thread.operation === "compact" &&
      thread.provider === "claude" &&
      live &&
      !live.compactConfirmed &&
      !live.compactWait &&
      !event.is_error &&
      !event.num_turns
    ) {
      live.compactWait = setTimeout(() => {
        live.compactWait = undefined;
        if (thread.operation === "compact" && !live.compactConfirmed)
          this.onEvent(thread, { ...event, is_error: true, subtype: "error" });
      }, 10 * 60_000);
      return true;
    }
    if (live?.compactWait) {
      clearTimeout(live.compactWait);
      live.compactWait = undefined;
    }
    return false;
  }

// ...
    thread: Thread,
    event: any,
    failure: string | undefined,
    failureCode: string | undefined,
  ) {
    const stop = event.is_error
// ...
      : undefined;
    if (!stop) return;
    const stopKey =
// ...
      provider: thread.provider,
      at: Date.now(),
      text: stop.text,
      resetsAt: stop.resetsAt ?? this.windowReset(stopKey),
    };
// ...
  }

  // Mark thread as quota-paused if turn ended due to rate limit and quota is exhausted
  private noteQuotaPause(thread: Thread, event: any, failure: string | undefined) {
    if (!event.is_error || thread.archived || thread.quotaPaused) return;
    const errMsg = [event.error?.message ?? event.error, event.result, failure]
      .filter((part) => typeof part === "string")
      .join("\n");
    if (!QUOTA_ERROR.test(errMsg)) return;
    const providerKey =
// ...
      .get(providerKey)
      ?.windows.find(
        (candidate) =>
          candidate.used >= candidate.limit &&
          candidate.resetsAt &&
          candidate.resetsAt > Date.now(),
      );
    if (!window) return;
    thread.quotaPaused = {
      provider: providerKey,
      windowLabel: window.label,
      resetsAt: window.resetsAt,
    };
    this.touch(thread);
  }

  // A /compact turn reports how it went; without compact_boundary it counts as failed
  private finishCompaction(thread: Thread, flow: EventFlow) {
    if (!flow.event.is_error && !this.live.get(thread.id)?.compactConfirmed) {
      flow.event = { ...flow.event, is_error: true, subtype: "error" };
      this.publish(thread.id, {
        type: "console_error",
        text: "The agent did not confirm compaction. Context has not been marked as compacted.",
      });
    }
    this.publish(thread.id, {
      type: "command_status",
      command: "compact",
      status: flow.event.is_error ? "failed" : "completed",
      text: flow.event.is_error
        ? "Context compaction failed."
        : "Context compacted. The conversation transcript is kept.",
    });
    delete thread.operation;
  }

  private notifyTurnEnd(thread: Thread, flow: EventFlow) {
    if (flow.compacting || !this.notificationTurns.has(thread.id)) return;
    if (!flow.event.is_error && thread.status !== "idle") return;
    const queued = !!this.queues.get(thread.id)?.length;
    this.notificationTurns.delete(thread.id);
    if (flow.event.is_error) this.notify(thread, "failed");
    else if (!queued && !this.questions.list(thread.id).length) this.notify(thread, "finished");
  }

  // An idle thread moves on: queued messages, waiting subagent results, then auto-continue. Each step may start a turn.
  private continueAfterResult(thread: Thread, event: any, unfinished: boolean) {
    if (
      thread.status === "idle" &&
      (!event.is_error ||
        parseCommand(this.queues.get(thread.id)?.[0]?.text || "")?.name === "compact")
    )
      this.drain(thread.id);
    if (thread.status === "idle" && !event.is_error) this.wake(thread);
    if (thread.status === "idle") this.autoContinue(thread, event, unfinished);
  }

  // Schedule, turn-done and issue-run hooks see an idle thread's finished turn last; a hook failure never breaks the turn
  private afterIdleResult(thread: Thread, flow: EventFlow) {
    const event = flow.event;
    if (event.type !== "result") return;
    if (thread.schedule && thread.status === "idle")
      try {
        this.onTurnEnd?.(thread, event);
      } catch {}
    if (thread.status === "idle")
      try {
        this.onTurnDone?.(thread, event);
      } catch {}
    if (thread.issueRun && thread.status === "idle")
      try {
        this.onIssueTurnEnd?.(
          thread,
          event.is_error ? { ...event, error: flow.issueError } : event,
        );
      } catch {}
  }

  // Ask the labeler for a short label of each tool call that needs one
  private noteAssistant(thread: Thread, event: any) {
    if (event.type !== "assistant") return;
    for (const block of event.message?.content ?? []) {
      if (block?.type !== "tool_use" || !needsLabel(block.name, block.input)) continue;
      this.labeler.request({
        thread: thread.id,
        id: block.id,
        name: block.name,
        command: toolSketch(block),
        cwd: thread.cwd,
      });
    }
  }

  // Background Bash calls count until they return.
  private trackBlocks(turn: TurnState, blocks: any[]) {
    for (const block of blocks) {
      if (block?.type !== "tool_use") continue;
      if (block.name === "Bash" && block.input?.run_in_background === true && block.id)
        (turn.bgTools ??= new Set()).add(String(block.id));
    }
  }

  private trackResults(turn: TurnState | undefined, content: any) {
    if (!turn?.bgTools?.size || !Array.isArray(content)) return;
    for (const block of content)
      if (block?.type === "tool_result") turn.bgTools.delete(String(block.tool_use_id));
  }

  private continueBlocked(thread: Thread, event: any, unfinished: boolean) {
    const live = this.live.get(thread.id);
    return (
      !!event.is_error ||
      thread.status !== "idle" ||
      !!thread.operation ||
      !!thread.background ||
      unfinished ||
      !!live?.wakes?.length ||
      !!this.queues.get(thread.id)?.length ||
      !!this.questions.list(thread.id).length
    );
  }

  private continueParked(thread: Thread) {
    return !!(
      thread.waiting ||
      thread.issueRun ||
      thread.schedule ||
      thread.quotaPaused ||
// ...
      thread.archived
    );
  }

  // Every user turn gets one "Continue" nudge when it ends idle; the nudge's own turn never re-arms.
  private autoContinue(thread: Thread, event: any, unfinished: boolean) {
    if (!this.continueArmed.has(thread.id)) return;
    if (Number(event.queued_turn_count) > 0) return;
    this.continueArmed.delete(thread.id);
    if (!autoContinueEnabled()) return;
    if (this.continueBlocked(thread, event, unfinished) || this.continueParked(thread)) return;
    const live = this.live.get(thread.id);
    setTimeout(() => {
      if (this.live.get(thread.id) !== live || this.threads.get(thread.id) !== thread) return;
      if (this.continueBlocked(thread, {}, false) || this.continueParked(thread)) return;
      try {
        this.send(thread.id, AUTO_CONTINUE_PROMPT, { auto: "continue" });
      } catch {}
    }, 250);
  }

  // Give finished subagent results to an agent that does not wake itself (Codex). Waits behind a running turn or queued messages.
  private wake(t: Thread, text?: string) {
    const live = this.live.get(t.id);
    if (!live) return;
    if (text) (live.wakes ??= []).push(text);
    if (!live.wakes?.length) return;
    setTimeout(() => {
      if (
        this.live.get(t.id) !== live ||
        t.status !== "idle" ||
        this.queues.get(t.id)?.length ||
        !live.wakes?.length
      )
        return;
      const msg = live.wakes.splice(0).join("\n\n");
      try {
        this.send(t.id, msg, { auto: "subagent" });
      } catch (e: any) {
        this.publish(t.id, {
          type: "console_error",
          text: `Could not resume after the subagent finished: ${e.message}`,
        });
      }
    }, 50);
  }

  private finishTurn(thread: Thread, event: any) {
// ...
      ? this.accounting.consume(event.session_id || thread.sessionId || thread.id, event)
      : undefined;
    if (measured?.duplicate) return;
    const turn = this.turns.get(thread.id);
    if (!turn) return;
    this.endTiming(thread, event);
    this.turns.delete(thread.id);
    const row = turnRow(thread, event, turn, measured);
// ...
    }
    try {
      recordTurn(row);
    } catch {}
  }

  // Close the turn's clock: one timing row, and the compact facts the Run details panel shows for the last run.
  private endTiming(t: Thread, ev: any) {
    const turn = this.turns.get(t.id),
      clock = turn?.clock;
    if (!clock) return;
    turn.clock = undefined;
    timed(() => {
      const live = this.live.get(t.id);
      const row = clock.finish(
        ev,
        Date.now(),
        live?.readyAt != null && live.spawnAt != null ? live.readyAt - live.spawnAt : null,
      );
      t.lastRun = { ...t.lastRun, timing: runFacts(row) };
      timing?.recordTurn(t.id, row);
    });
  }

  private command(thread: Thread, text: string, attachments?: unknown): boolean {
    const parsed = /^\/\s*$/.test(text.trim()) ? { name: "help", args: "" } : parseCommand(text);
    if (!parsed) return false;
    const { name, args } = parsed;
    if (!CONSOLE_COMMANDS.some((entry) => entry.name === name)) {
// ...
      throw new Error(
        `/${name} is not available for this agent. Type /help for supported commands.`,
      );
    }
    if (Array.isArray(attachments) && attachments.length)
      throw new Error("Run commands without attachments.");
    if (name === "stop") {
      this.abort(thread.id);
      return true;
    }
    if (name === "compact") {
      this.compact(thread.id, args);
      return true;
    }
    this.applyCommandSetting(thread, name, args);
    this.publish(thread.id, {
      type: "command_status",
      command: name,
      status: "completed",
      text: this.commandReply(thread, name),
    });
    return true;
  }

  private applyCommandSetting(thread: Thread, name: string, args: string) {
    if (name === "model" && args) {
      if (!PROVIDERS.get(thread.provider)?.models().includes(args))
        throw new Error("Unknown model. Choose one from the model menu.");
      this.update(thread.id, { model: args });
    } else if (name === "effort" && args) this.update(thread.id, { effort: args });
  }

  private commandReply(thread: Thread, name: string): string {
    if (name === "help")
      return this.getCommands()
        [thread.provider].map(
          (entry) =>
            `/${entry.name}${entry.argumentHint ? " " + entry.argumentHint : ""} — ${entry.description}`,
        )
        .join("\n");
    const used = thread.context?.used;
    return `${PROVIDERS.get(thread.provider)?.label}: ${thread.model} · effort ${thread.effort || "default"} · ${thread.operation === "compact" ? "compacting" : thread.status}${used != null ? ` · ${used.toLocaleString()} context tokens` : ""}`;
  }

  compact(id: string, instructions = "") {
    const t = this.threads.get(id);
    if (!t) throw new Error("Unknown thread");
    if (t.provider === "codex" && instructions)
      throw new Error("Codex /compact does not accept extra instructions.");
    if (
      t.operation === "compact" ||
      this.queues.get(id)?.some((q) => parseCommand(q.text)?.name === "compact")
    )
      throw new Error("Context compaction is already running or queued.");
    if (t.status === "running")
      return this.whileRunning(t, `/compact${instructions ? " " + instructions : ""}`, {
        mode: "queue",
      });
    if (!fs.existsSync(logFile(id)) || (t.provider === "codex" && !t.sessionId))
      throw new Error("Send a message before compacting this thread.");
// ...
    const live = this.start(t);
    if (!live.agent.compact) {
      this.kill(id);
      throw new Error("This agent does not support context compaction.");
    }
    live.compactConfirmed = false;
    t.operation = "compact";
    t.status = "running";
    this.publish(id, {
      type: "user_input",
      text: `/compact${instructions ? " " + instructions : ""}`,
      command: true,
      ts: Date.now(),
    });
    this.publish(id, {
      type: "command_status",
      command: "compact",
      status: "running",
      text: "Compacting context…",
    });
    this.turns.set(id, {
      start: Date.now(),
      tools: {},
      clock: new TurnClock({ provider: t.provider, alias: t.model, start: Date.now(), cold: true }),
    });
    this.touch(t);
    try {
      live.agent.compact(instructions);
    } catch (error) {
      this.kill(id);
      delete t.operation;
      t.status = "idle";
      this.touch(t);
      throw error;
    }
  }

  // `attachments` is untrusted browser input: [{id}] naming files already uploaded to this thread
  // While a turn is running a message either steers it (delivered to the agent now, picked up after its current tool call) or waits in
  // a queue for the turn to end. `mode: "queue"` forces the queue; so does an agent that cannot take input mid-turn.
  // `instructions` go to the agent after the text but stay out of the transcript bubble; `scheduled` labels a scheduled run's prompt.
  send(threadId: string, text: string, opts: SendOptions = {}) {
    const thread = this.threads.get(threadId);
    if (!thread) throw new Error("Unknown thread");
    const attachments = this.prepareSend(thread, text, opts);
    if (!attachments) return;
    if (thread.status === "running") {
      if (opts.auto) throw new Error("A turn is already running.");
      return this.whileRunning(thread, text, opts);
    }
    // older queued messages are about to drain (or wait on an edit): a new one joins behind them, never jumps ahead
    if (!opts.auto && !opts.delivery && this.queues.get(threadId)?.length) {
      this.enqueue(thread, text, opts);
      this.drain(threadId);
      return "queued";
    }
    const prompt = this.composePrompt(thread, text, opts, attachments);
    const live = this.beginTurn(thread, opts);
    this.publish(threadId, userInputEvent(text, opts, attachments));
    this.touch(thread);
    if (live.held) this.release(thread, live); // a warm agent's start-up events, after the message as on a cold start
    if (opts.auto) this.notificationTurns.delete(threadId);
    else this.notificationTurns.add(threadId);
    try {
      live.agent.send(
        prompt,
        attachments.filter((attachment) => attachment.kind === "image"),
      );
    } catch (error) {
      if (this.notificationTurns.delete(threadId)) this.notify(thread, "failed");
      throw error;
    }
    if (!opts.auto && !opts.sourceThread) this.notePrompt(thread, text);
    return "started";
  }

  private prepareSend(thread: Thread, text: string, opts: SendOptions) {
    if (
      !opts.sourceThread &&
      !opts.auto &&
      !opts.questionAnswer &&
      this.command(thread, text, opts.attachments)
    )
      return;
    const attachments = resolveAttachments(thread.id, opts.attachments);
    if (!opts.sourceThread) this.answerAsyncQuestions(thread, text, opts, attachments);
    if (!opts.auto && !opts.sourceThread) this.noteUserActivity(thread);
    return attachments;
  }

  authorizeThreadTool(threadId: string, name: string, input: unknown) {
    if (!this.live.has(threadId) || this.threads.get(threadId)?.status !== "running")
      return Promise.resolve(false);
    return this.threadToolAuth.consume(threadId, name, input);
  }

  async threadTool(name: string, senderId: string | undefined, input: any) {
    const sender = senderId ? this.threads.get(senderId) : undefined;
    if (
      !sender ||
      sender.archived ||
      !senderId ||
      !this.live.has(senderId) ||
      sender.status !== "running"
    )
      throw new Error("Thread tools require a live Agent Console thread.");
    if (name === "thread_list") {
      if (!input || typeof input !== "object" || Array.isArray(input) || Object.keys(input).length)
        throw new Error("thread_list takes no arguments.");
      return {
        threads: [...this.threads.values()]
          .filter((thread) => !thread.archived)
          .map(({ id: threadId, title, provider, status }) => ({
            id: threadId,
            title,
            provider,
            status,
          })),
      };
    }
    if (name !== "thread_send") throw new Error("Unknown thread tool.");
    if (!(await this.authorizeThreadTool(senderId, name, input)))
      throw new Error("thread_send is available only to the top-level agent's current tool call.");
    if (sender.archived || sender.status !== "running" || !this.live.has(senderId))
      throw new Error("Sending thread is no longer running.");
    return this.sendThreadMessage(sender, input);
  }

  private sendThreadMessage(sender: Thread, input: any) {
    if (
      !input ||
      typeof input !== "object" ||
      Array.isArray(input) ||
      Object.keys(input).some((key) => !["thread", "message"].includes(key)) ||
      typeof input.thread !== "string" ||
      typeof input.message !== "string" ||
      !input.message.trim() ||
      input.message.length > 20_000
    )
      throw new Error(
        "Use thread_send with a thread id and a nonempty message of at most 20000 characters.",
      );
    const target = this.threads.get(input.thread);
    if (!target || target.archived)
      throw new Error("Target thread is missing or archived. Call thread_list.");
    if (target.id === sender.id) throw new Error("Cannot send a message to the same thread.");
    const sourceThread = { id: sender.id, title: sender.title, provider: sender.provider };
    const text = `[Message from thread ${sender.id} (${sender.provider}): ${sender.title}]\n${input.message}`;
    const delivery = this.send(target.id, text, { sourceThread });
    return { thread: target.id, delivery };
  }

  // A plain chat reply answers the thread's pending background questions.
  private answerAsyncQuestions(
    thread: Thread,
    text: string,
    opts: SendOptions,
    attachments: ReturnType<typeof resolveAttachments>,
  ) {
    if (opts.auto || opts.delivery || opts.questionAnswer) return;
    if (!text.trim() || attachments.length) return;
    if (thread.status === "running" && opts.mode === "queue") return;
    for (const question of this.questions.list(thread.id).filter((request) => request.async)) {
      const result = this.questions.answer(
        thread.id,
        question.id,
        {},
        undefined,
        false,
        text,
        () => false,
      );
      this.publish(thread.id, result.event);
      this.updateWaiting(thread.id);
    }
  }

  private noteUserActivity(thread: Thread) {
    this.continueArmed.add(thread.id);
    delete thread.quotaPaused; // the user continued by hand: no auto "Continue." later
    thread.lastUserActivityAt = Math.max(Date.now(), (thread.lastUserActivityAt ?? 0) + 1);
// ...
  }

  private composePrompt(
    thread: Thread,
    text: string,
    opts: SendOptions,
    attachments: ReturnType<typeof resolveAttachments>,
  ): string {
    const prompt =
      withFileList(text, attachments) + (opts.instructions ? `\n\n---\n${opts.instructions}` : "");
    if (!thread.replay || opts.auto) return prompt;
    const preamble =
      thread.replayLimit === 6000
        ? recentPreamble(this.history(thread.id))
        : replayPreamble(this.history(thread.id)); // exclude this new message
    thread.replay = false;
    delete thread.replayLimit;
    return preamble + prompt;
  }

  // Get an agent ready for the turn: reuse the warm one or start cold, then open the turn's clock.
  private beginTurn(thread: Thread, opts: SendOptions) {
// ...
    if (!opts.auto) delete thread.archived; // writing to an archived thread puts it back to work (touch below persists it)
    this.recycleRouting(thread.id);
    let live = this.live.get(thread.id);
    if (live?.interrupting) {
      this.kill(thread.id, "stop");
      live = undefined;
    } // its interrupted turn has not ended yet
    const cold = !live || !live.agent.alive; // a pre-warmed agent counts as warm
    const start = Date.now();
    if (cold) live = this.start(thread);
    live = live!;
    live.aborted = false;
    thread.status = "running";
    this.turns.set(thread.id, {
      start,
      tools: {},
      clock: new TurnClock({
        provider: thread.provider,
        alias: thread.model,
        start,
        cold,
        model: live.model,
        queueMs: opts.queuedAt != null ? Math.max(0, start - opts.queuedAt) : undefined,
      }),
    });
    return live;
  }

  // Record a user prompt for naming. Every prompt with substance (re)names the thread unless the user chose its title. Media is not
  // part of `text`, so an image-only message names nothing; "ok" / "hi" say nothing about the topic either.
  private notePrompt(t: Thread, text: string) {
    const clean = titleText(text);
    if (!clean) return;
    if (clean.split(" ").length < 3) return;
    if (t.autoTitle) t.autoTitle = false; // the folder-name default is gone, whatever the outcome
    if (!t.titleLocked) void this.retitle(t, clean);
  }

  private whileRunning(thread: Thread, text: string, opts: SendOptions) {
    const attachments = resolveAttachments(thread.id, opts.attachments); // reject bad ids now, not when the queue drains
    const agent = this.live.get(thread.id)?.agent;
    // A normal chat reply also releases a waiting tool; otherwise steering can sit behind that tool forever.
    if (
      !opts.sourceThread &&
      opts.mode !== "queue" &&
      !attachments.length &&
      text.trim() &&
      this.answerBlockingQuestions(thread, text, agent)
    )
      return;
    if (thread.operation !== "compact" && opts.mode !== "queue" && agent?.steer) {
      return this.steerAgent(thread, text, opts, attachments, agent);
    }
    this.enqueue(thread, text, opts);
    return "queued";
  }

  // True when a waiting tool consumed the reply.
  private answerBlockingQuestions(thread: Thread, text: string, agent: Agent | undefined): boolean {
    for (const question of this.questions.list(thread.id).filter((request) => !request.async)) {
      const result = this.questions.answer(thread.id, question.id, {}, agent, false, text);
      this.publish(thread.id, result.event);
      this.updateWaiting(thread.id);
      if (result.consumed) return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  private steerAgent(
    thread: Thread,
    text: string,
    opts: SendOptions,
    attachments: ReturnType<typeof resolveAttachments>,
    agent: Agent,
  ) {
    const delivery = agent.steer!(
      withFileList(text, attachments),
      attachments.filter((attachment) => attachment.kind === "image"),
    );
    if (!(delivery instanceof Promise)) return this.steerAccepted(thread, text, opts, attachments);
    void delivery
      .then(() => this.steerAccepted(thread, text, opts, attachments))
      .catch((error) => {
        if (!this.threads.has(thread.id) || this.live.get(thread.id)?.agent !== agent) return;
        this.steerRejected(thread, text, opts, error);
      });
    return "steering";
  }

  private steerAccepted(
    thread: Thread,
    text: string,
    opts: SendOptions,
    attachments: ReturnType<typeof resolveAttachments>,
  ) {
    if (!opts.sourceThread) this.continueArmed.add(thread.id);
    this.notificationTurns.add(thread.id);
    if (!opts.sourceThread) this.notePrompt(thread, text);
    this.publish(thread.id, {
      type: "user_input",
      text,
      ts: Date.now(),
      delivery: "steer",
      ...(opts.questionAnswer ? { questionAnswer: true } : {}),
      ...(opts.sourceThread ? { sourceThread: opts.sourceThread } : {}),
      ...attachmentSummary(attachments),
    });
    return "steered";
  }

  private steerRejected(thread: Thread, text: string, opts: SendOptions, error: Error) {
    this.publish(thread.id, {
      type: "console_error",
      text: `Steering was not accepted: ${error.message}. Sending as the next message.`,
    });
    try {
      this.send(thread.id, text, { ...opts, mode: "queue" });
    } catch (failure: any) {
      this.publish(thread.id, {
        type: "console_error",
        text: `Message could not be sent: ${failure.message}`,
      });
    }
  }

  private enqueue(thread: Thread, text: string, opts: SendOptions) {
    const queue = this.queues.get(thread.id) ?? [];
    if (queue.length >= MAX_QUEUE) throw new Error("Too many queued messages.");
    queue.push({
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      text,
      attachments: opts.attachments,
      revision: 0,
      at: Date.now(),
      ...(opts.questionAnswer ? { questionAnswer: true } : {}),
      ...(opts.sourceThread ? { sourceThread: opts.sourceThread } : {}),
    });
    this.queues.set(thread.id, queue);
    this.emitQueue(thread.id);
  }

  private emitQueue(id: string) {
    fs.writeFileSync(QUEUES_FILE + ".tmp", JSON.stringify(Object.fromEntries(this.queues)), {
      mode: 0o600,
    });
    fs.renameSync(QUEUES_FILE + ".tmp", QUEUES_FILE);
    this.emit({ t: "queue", thread: id, items: (this.queues.get(id) ?? []).map(queuePreview) });
  }
  getQueues(): Record<string, ReturnType<typeof queuePreview>[]> {
    const out: Record<string, ReturnType<typeof queuePreview>[]> = {};
    for (const [id, q] of this.queues) if (q.length) out[id] = q.map(queuePreview);
    return out;
  }
  editQueued(id: string, qid: string) {
    const item = this.queues.get(id)?.find((q) => q.id === qid);
    if (!item) throw new Error("This message is no longer queued. It may already have been sent.");
    item.editing = true;
    this.emitQueue(id);
    return {
      id: item.id,
      text: item.text,
      revision: item.revision,
      attachments: Array.isArray(item.attachments) ? item.attachments.length : 0,
    };
  }
  saveQueued(id: string, qid: string, revision: number, text: unknown) {
    const item = this.queues.get(id)?.find((q) => q.id === qid);
    if (!item)
      throw new Error("This message is no longer queued. Your draft has been kept in the editor.");
    if (!item.editing || item.revision !== revision)
      throw new Error(
        "This queued message changed in another window. Reopen it to edit the latest version.",
      );
    if (
      typeof text !== "string" ||
      text.length > 100_000 ||
      (!text.trim() && !(Array.isArray(item.attachments) && item.attachments.length))
    )
      throw new Error("Enter a message of up to 100,000 characters, or keep an attachment.");
    item.text = text;
    item.revision++;
    item.editing = false;
    this.emitQueue(id);
    this.drain(id);
  }
  cancelQueuedEdit(id: string, qid: string, revision: number) {
    const item = this.queues.get(id)?.find((q) => q.id === qid);
    if (item && item.revision === revision) {
      item.editing = false;
      this.emitQueue(id);
      this.drain(id);
    }
  }
  // "Steer" on a queued card: hand that one message to the running turn now instead of waiting for it to end
  steerQueued(id: string, qid: string) {
    const t = this.threads.get(id);
    const q = this.queues.get(id);
    const item = q?.find((x) => x.id === qid);
    if (!t || !item)
      throw new Error("This message is no longer queued. It may already have been sent.");
    if (item.editing) throw new Error("Finish editing this message first.");
    if (t.status !== "running") return this.drain(id);
    if (t.operation || !this.live.get(id)?.agent.steer)
      throw new Error(
        "This agent cannot take a message mid-turn. It will be sent when the turn ends.",
      );
    this.queues.set(
      id,
      q!.filter((x) => x !== item),
    );
    this.emitQueue(id);
    this.whileRunning(t, item.text, {
      attachments: item.attachments,
      mode: "steer",
      ...(item.questionAnswer ? { questionAnswer: true } : {}),
      ...(item.sourceThread ? { sourceThread: item.sourceThread } : {}),
    });
  }
  unqueue(id: string, qid: string) {
    const q = this.queues.get(id);
    if (!q) return;
    this.queues.set(
      id,
      q.filter((x) => x.id !== qid),
    );
    this.emitQueue(id);
    this.drain(id);
  }
  // When a turn ends leaving the thread idle, everything queued behind it goes out together as ONE prompt. A command such as
  // /compact always goes alone, and a message being edited holds back the ones after it.
  private drain(id: string) {
    if (!this.queues.get(id)?.length) return;
    setTimeout(() => {
      const t = this.threads.get(id);
      const q = this.queues.get(id);
      if (!t || t.status !== "idle" || !q?.length || q[0].editing) return;
      let n = 1;
      let files = attachList(q[0]).length;
      if (!q[0].sourceThread && !parseCommand(q[0].text)) {
        while (
          n < q.length &&
          !q[n].editing &&
          !q[n].sourceThread &&
          !parseCommand(q[n].text) &&
          files + attachList(q[n]).length <= MAX_ATTACHMENTS
        )
          files += attachList(q[n++]).length;
      }
      const items = q.splice(0, n);
      const { text, attachments, questionAnswer, sourceThread } = mergeQueued(items);
      const queuedAt = Math.min(...items.map((x) => x.at ?? Infinity));
      this.emitQueue(id);
      try {
        this.send(id, text, {
          attachments,
          delivery: "queued",
          ...(questionAnswer ? { questionAnswer: true } : {}),
          ...(sourceThread ? { sourceThread } : {}),
          ...(Number.isFinite(queuedAt) ? { queuedAt } : {}),
        });
      } catch (e: any) {
        this.publish(id, {
          type: "console_error",
          text: `A queued message could not be sent: ${e?.message ?? e}`,
        });
      }
    }, 250).unref();
  }

  // Every typed user message of the thread, oldest first. The log is read only for its user_input lines.
  /** Record progress on an issue run (its PR, that its review has started). */
  setIssueRun(id: string, patch: Partial<NonNullable<Thread["issueRun"]>>) {
    const t = this.threads.get(id);
    if (!t?.issueRun) return;
    t.issueRun = { ...t.issueRun, ...patch };
    this.touch(t);
  }

  private async userTexts(id: string): Promise<string[]> {
    const texts: string[] = [];
    try {
      for await (const l of readLines(logFile(id))) {
        if (!l.includes('"user_input"')) continue;
        try {
          const e = JSON.parse(l);
          if (
            e.type === "user_input" &&
            !e.auto &&
            !e.sourceThread &&
            !e.command &&
            typeof e.text === "string"
          )
            texts.push(e.text);
        } catch {}
      }
    } catch {}
    return texts;
  }

  // Name the thread after all its user messages (newest weigh most). Never blocks the turn; on any failure the current title stays.
  private async retitle(t: Thread, latest: string) {
    const n = (this.titling.get(t.id) ?? 0) + 1;
    this.titling.set(t.id, n);
    const texts = await this.userTexts(t.id);
// ...
    if (!title || !this.threads.has(t.id) || t.titleLocked || this.titling.get(t.id) !== n) return; // deleted, renamed by the user, or a newer prompt is already being named
    t.title = title;
    this.touch(t);
  }

  abort(id: string) {
    this.threadToolAuth.clear(id);
    this.notificationTurns.delete(id);
    this.continueArmed.delete(id);
    const t = this.threads.get(id);
    const live = this.live.get(id);
    live?.foreground?.clear();
    live?.backgroundTools?.clear();
    live?.taskTools?.clear();
    if (!t) return;
    this.cancelQuestions(id, true);
// ...
    this.persist();
    if (this.queues.delete(id)) this.emitQueue(id); // Stop means stop: nothing queued behind it runs
    if (!live) {
      delete t.background;
      this.emitWorkState(t);
      this.touch(t);
      return;
    }
    this.endTiming(t, { subtype: "aborted", is_error: true });
    // interrupt keeps the process, and with it any background subagents: stop with subagents working must kill the agent
    if (!t.operation && !t.background && live.agent.interrupt && live.agent.alive && !live.held) {
      // keep the process: the harness ends the turn itself; restart only if it does not within 3 s
      live.interrupting = setTimeout(() => {
        if (this.live.get(id) === live && live.interrupting) this.kill(id, "stop", true);
      }, 3000);
      live.interrupting.unref();
      live.agent.interrupt();
    } else {
      live.aborted = true;
      live.exitReason = "stop";
      live.rewarm = !t.archived;
      this.live.delete(id); // next send uses a fresh agent that resumes the session
      live.agent.abort();
    }
    t.status = "idle";
    delete t.operation;
    delete t.background;
    this.emitWorkState(t);
    this.publish(id, { type: "aborted", ts: Date.now() });
    this.touch(t);
  }

  private kill(id: string, reason = "kill", rewarm = false) {
    this.threadToolAuth.clear(id);
    this.notificationTurns.delete(id);
    this.continueArmed.delete(id);
    this.cancelQuestions(id);
    const live = this.live.get(id);
    if (live) {
      live.exitReason ??= reason;
      clearTimeout(live.interrupting);
      live.rewarm = rewarm && live.agent.alive;
      live.aborted = true;
      this.live.delete(id);
      live.agent.kill();
    }
    const t = this.threads.get(id);
    if (t) this.emitWorkState(t);
    if (t?.background) {
      delete t.background;
      this.emit({ t: "thread", thread: t });
    } // the tasks died with the process
  }

  private staleRouting(live: Live) {
// ...
  }

  private recycleRouting(id: string) {
    const live = this.live.get(id),
      t = this.threads.get(id);
    if (!live || !t || !this.staleRouting(live)) return false;
    live.recycleRouting = true;
    // Background subagents and running turns retain their process until they finish.
    if (t.status !== "idle" || t.background || t.operation) return false;
    this.kill(id, "stale-proxy-routing");
    return true;
  }

  routingHealth() {
    let idle = 0,
      active = 0,
      silent = 0;
    for (const [id, live] of this.live) {
      const t = this.threads.get(id);
      if (!t) continue;
      if (this.staleRouting(live)) {
        if (t.status === "idle" && !t.background && !t.operation) idle++;
        else active++;
      }
      if (this.staleTurn(t, live)) silent++;
    }
    return { idle, active, silent };
  }

  private staleTurn(t: Thread, live: Live) {
    return (
      t.status === "running" &&
      !t.background &&
      !this.questions.list(t.id).some((q) => !q.async) &&
      Date.now() -
        Math.max(live.heardAt ?? 0, live.spawnAt ?? 0, this.turns.get(t.id)?.start ?? 0) >
        TURN_STALE_MS
    );
  }

  repairStaleTurns() {
    let fixed = 0;
    for (const [id, live] of this.live) {
      const t = this.threads.get(id);
      if (t && this.staleTurn(t, live)) {
        this.expire(t);
        fixed++;
      }
    }
    return fixed;
  }

  repairRouting() {
    let fixed = 0,
      deferred = 0;
    for (const [id, live] of this.live) {
      if (!this.staleRouting(live)) continue;
      if (this.recycleRouting(id)) fixed++;
      else deferred++;
    }
    return { fixed, deferred };
  }

  // free memory from idle agent processes; sessions resume on the next message
  sweep(memAvailable = os.freemem() / os.totalmem()) {
    this.repairRouting();
    const idle: [string, number][] = [];
    for (const [threadId, live] of this.live) this.sweepLive(threadId, live, idle);
    idle.sort((left, right) => left[1] - right[1]); // oldest first
    this.evictIdle(idle, memAvailable);
    // no process left to finish them
    for (const thread of this.threads.values())
      if (thread.background && !this.live.has(thread.id)) {
        delete thread.background;
        this.emit({ t: "thread", thread });
      }
  }

  private evictIdle(idle: [string, number][], memAvailable: number) {
    const keep = memAvailable < IDLE_MEM_FLOOR ? 2 : IDLE_MAX;
    for (const [threadId] of idle.slice(0, Math.max(0, idle.length - keep)))
      this.kill(threadId, memAvailable < IDLE_MEM_FLOOR ? "memory" : "idle-cap");
  }

  // One live agent: expire a hung turn, retire a stale process, or list it as an idle candidate.
  private sweepLive(threadId: string, live: Live, idle: [string, number][]) {
    const thread = this.threads.get(threadId);
    if (!thread) return;
    if (thread.status === "running") return this.sweepRunning(threadId, live, thread);
    if (thread.status !== "idle") return;
    if (thread.background) {
      if (Date.now() - (live.heardAt ?? live.spawnAt!) > BACKGROUND_STALE_MS)
        this.kill(threadId, "stale-background");
      return;
    }
    const lastActive = Math.max(thread.updatedAt, live.spawnAt!);
    if (Date.now() - lastActive > (live.warm ? WARM_IDLE_MS : IDLE_KILL_MS))
      return this.kill(threadId, "idle");
    if (!thread.operation) idle.push([threadId, lastActive]);
  }

  private sweepRunning(threadId: string, live: Live, thread: Thread) {
    if (this.questions.list(threadId).some((request) => !request.async)) live.heardAt = Date.now(); // waiting on the user is not silence
    const lastSign = Math.max(
      live.heardAt ?? 0,
      live.spawnAt!,
      this.turns.get(threadId)?.start ?? 0,
    );
    if (Date.now() - lastSign > TURN_STALE_MS) this.expire(thread);
  }

  // End a hung turn like a crash, except that whatever was queued behind it still goes out.
  private expire(t: Thread) {
    const notify = !t.operation && this.notificationTurns.has(t.id);
    this.kill(t.id, "stale-turn");
    t.status = "idle";
    delete t.operation;
    this.publish(t.id, {
      type: "console_error",
      text: `No output for ${Math.round(TURN_STALE_MS / 60_000)} minutes, so the hung turn was stopped. Send a message to continue the session.`,
    });
    if (notify) this.notify(t, "failed");
    this.endTiming(t, { subtype: "stale", is_error: true });
    this.touch(t);
    this.drain(t.id);
  }

  shutdown() {
    this.closed = true;
// ...
    for (const id of [...this.live.keys()]) this.kill(id, "shutdown");
  }
}


================
File: server/tool-priority.ts
================
import fs from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import { DATA_DIR } from "./auth.ts";

/* Agents' tool work (tests, builds, browsers, scratch servers) runs at a lower CPU priority than the agents themselves and this
   server, so a busy box delays background work instead of the next model step or a chat stream. Two layers:
   - Claude sources CLAUDE_ENV_FILE before every Bash command, so each command starts at TOOL_NICE (verified on CLI 2.1).
   - A sweep renices every process two or more levels below this server: tool shells and MCP servers of every harness.
     The harness processes (direct children) keep normal priority. Lowering priority needs no privilege; raising it does,
     so nothing here can make a process faster than default. AGENT_CONSOLE_TOOL_NICE=0 turns it off. */
const PROC = "/proc";
export const TOOL_NICE = Math.max(
  0,
  Math.min(19, Number(process.env.AGENT_CONSOLE_TOOL_NICE ?? 10)),
);
const SWEEP_MS = 2000;

let envFile: string | null = null;
export function toolEnvFile(): string | null {
  if (!TOOL_NICE) return null;
  if (envFile) return envFile;
  const file = path.join(DATA_DIR, "tool-env.sh");
  try {
    fs.writeFileSync(
      file,
      `# Written by agent-console: Bash tool commands run below the agent's own priority.\nrenice -n ${TOOL_NICE} -p $$ >/dev/null 2>&1\n`,
      { mode: 0o644 },
    );
    envFile = file;
  } catch {}
  return envFile;
}

function children(pid: number): number[] {
  const out: number[] = [];
  let tasks: string[] = [];
  try {
    tasks = fs.readdirSync(path.join(PROC, String(pid), "task"));
  } catch {
    return out;
  }
  for (const tid of tasks) {
    try {
      for (const c of fs
        .readFileSync(path.join(PROC, String(pid), "task", tid, "children"), "utf8")
        .split(" "))
        if (c.trim()) out.push(Number(c));
    } catch {}
  }
  return out;
}

/* Renice descendants below the direct children of `root`. Returns how many processes were lowered. */
export function lowerToolPriority(root = process.pid, nice = TOOL_NICE): number {
  let lowered = 0;
  const seen = new Set<number>();
  let level = children(root);
  for (let depth = 1; level.length && depth < 32; depth++) {
    const next: number[] = [];
    for (const pid of level) {
      if (seen.has(pid)) continue;
      seen.add(pid);
      if (depth >= 2) {
        try {
          if (os.getPriority(pid) < nice) {
            os.setPriority(pid, nice);
            lowered++;
          }
        } catch {}
      }
      next.push(...children(pid));
    }
    level = next;
  }
  return lowered;
}

let started = false;
export function startToolPriority() {
  if (started || !TOOL_NICE) return;
  started = true;
  toolEnvFile();
  setInterval(() => lowerToolPriority(), SWEEP_MS).unref();
}

// Filtered excerpt: some source lines are omitted.
import fs from "node:fs";
import { DatabaseSync } from "node:sqlite";

// Per-turn timing and agent-process lifecycle, measured by the server from the canonical event stream it already receives.
// Metadata only: tool names, durations, counts, HTTP statuses and short error classes. Never prompt, command or output text.
// Nothing here reaches a model or the network. Stored next to the browser samples in latency.sqlite (bounded rows, day window, 0600)
// ...

export const RETENTION_DAYS = 30;
const MAX_TOOLS = 100,
  MAX_RETRIES = 20;
// Timeline: ordered segments of one turn for the Run details "Full breakdown". Bounded: the first MAX_SEGMENTS in time order,
// everything later folded into one tail summary. Tool spans are tracked up to MAX_TIMED_TOOLS (the tools column keeps MAX_TOOLS).
export const MAX_SEGMENTS = 300;
const MAX_TIMED_TOOLS = 400;

const toolName = (s: unknown) =>
  (typeof s === "string" && s.replace(/[^\w.:-]/g, "").slice(0, 64)) || "tool";
const shortWord = (s: unknown) =>
  typeof s === "string" && /^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{0,39}$/.test(s) ? s : undefined;

// A raw provider error becomes one short class. The message itself is never kept.
const CLASSES: [RegExp, string][] = [
// ...
  [/\b529\b|overload/i, "overloaded"],
  [/\b40[13]\b|unauthori[sz]ed|forbidden|authentication/i, "auth"],
  [/time.?out|timed out|ETIMEDOUT/i, "timeout"],
  [/ECONN|ENOTFOUND|EAI_AGAIN|socket|network|fetch failed|disconnect/i, "network"],
  [/\b5\d\d\b|server error|internal error|bad gateway|unavailable/i, "server_error"],
  [/\b4\d\d\b|invalid request|bad request/i, "bad_request"],
];
export function errorClass(raw: unknown): string {
  if (typeof raw !== "string" || !raw.trim()) return "unknown";
  const word = shortWord(
    raw
      .trim()
      .replace(/([a-z])([A-Z])/g, "$1_$2")
      .toLowerCase(),
  ); // Claude "rate_limit", Codex "serverOverloaded"
  if (word) return word;
  for (const [re, name] of CLASSES) if (re.test(raw)) return name;
  return "other";
}
export function httpStatus(n: unknown, text?: unknown): number | null {
  const v = Number(n);
  if (n != null && Number.isInteger(v) && v >= 100 && v <= 599) return v;
  const m = typeof text === "string" ? /\b([45]\d\d)\b/.exec(text) : null;
  return m ? Number(m[1]) : null;
}
// ...
export function retryEvent(o: {
  attempt?: unknown;
  status?: unknown;
  error?: unknown;
  message?: unknown;
  delay?: unknown;
}) {
  return {
    type: "system",
    subtype: "api_retry",
    attempt: Number.isInteger(o.attempt) ? o.attempt : null,
    error_status: httpStatus(o.status, o.message),
    error: o.error != null ? errorClass(String(o.error)) : errorClass(o.message),
    ...(Number.isFinite(o.delay) ? { retry_delay_ms: o.delay } : {}),
  };
}

interface Span {
  a: number;
  b?: number;
}
function covered(spans: Span[], end: number) {
  const xs = spans.map((s) => [s.a, Math.max(s.a, s.b ?? end)]).sort((x, y) => x[0] - y[0]);
  let total = 0,
    a = -Infinity,
    b = -Infinity;
  for (const [x, y] of xs) {
    if (x > b) {
      if (b > a) total += b - a;
      a = x;
      b = y;
    } else b = Math.max(b, y);
  }
  if (b > a) total += b - a;
  return total;
}
// Events the adapter itself makes up front; they say nothing about the process or the model.
const SYNTHETIC = new Set(["run_settings", "console_error"]);
export const fromProcess = (ev: any) => !SYNTHETIC.has(ev?.type);

export interface ToolTime {
  n: string;
  ms: number;
  e?: 1;
}
// a/d: start offset and duration in ms from the turn start (queue sits before 0). i: model step number.
// model f: that step's time to first output. retry s/c: HTTP status and short error class. tool e: failed.
export type Segment =
  | { k: "queue" | "startup" | "wait"; a: number; d: number }
  | { k: "model"; i: number; a: number; d: number; f: number | null }
  | { k: "tool"; i: number; n: string; a: number; d: number; e?: 1 }
  | { k: "retry"; i: number; a: number; d: number; s: number | null; c: string };
export interface Timeline {
  seg: Segment[];
  more?: {
    n: number;
    from: number;
    model_ms: number;
    tool_ms: number;
    other_ms: number;
    tools: number;
    errors: number;
  };
}
export interface TurnTiming {
  turn_ts: number;
  provider: string;
  model: string;
  alias: string;
  cold: 0 | 1;
  spawn_ms: number | null;
  queue_ms: number | null;
  first_ms: number | null;
  total_ms: number;
  model_ms: number;
  tool_ms: number;
  startup_ms: number;
  wait_ms: number;
  steps: number;
  tools: ToolTime[];
  retries: number;
  retry_detail: { s: number | null; c: string }[];
  rate_limits: number;
  outcome: string;
  timeline?: Timeline;
}

// One turn, as the server sees it: send -> first process event (startup) -> first model output (TTFT) -> tools <-> model steps -> result.
// Model time is what is left after tools, user waits (questions) and startup; API retries and their delays count as model time.
// Main-thread only: a subagent's own tools run inside its Agent tool call and are counted there.
export class TurnClock {
  private firstEvent?: number;
  private firstOutput?: number;
  private tools = new Map<string, Span & { n: string; e?: 1; i: number; q: number }>();
  private stepSpans: { a: number; first?: number; last?: number; q: number }[] = [];
  private retrySegs: { a: number; d: number; s: number | null; c: string; i: number; q: number }[] =
    [];
  private seq = 0; // arrival order: breaks ties between events in the same millisecond
  private lastResult?: number;
  private waits = new Map<string, Span & { q: number }>();
  private steps = 0;
  private inModel = false;
  private model?: string;
  private retries: { s: number | null; c: string }[] = [];
  private retryCount = 0;
  private rateLimits = 0;
  private o: {
    provider: string;
    alias: string;
    start: number;
    cold: boolean;
    queueMs?: number;
    model?: string;
  };
  // model: the id this process already resolved (harnesses that announce it once per process, e.g. Codex)
  constructor(o: {
    provider: string;
    alias: string;
    start: number;
    cold: boolean;
    queueMs?: number;
    model?: string;
  }) {
    this.o = o;
    this.model = o.model;
  }
  get start() {
    return this.o.start;
  }
  get resolvedModel() {
    return this.model;
  }
  private output(now: number) {
    this.firstOutput ??= now;
    if (!this.inModel) {
      this.steps++;
      this.inModel = true;
      // A step's request goes out when the last tool result lands (or, for the first step, when the process first speaks).
      const a = this.steps === 1 ? (this.firstEvent ?? this.o.start) : (this.lastResult ?? now);
      this.stepSpans.push({ a: Math.min(a, now), first: now, q: ++this.seq });
    }
    this.stepSpans[this.stepSpans.length - 1].last = now;
  }
  private noteRetry(event: any, now: number) {
    this.retryCount++;
    const status = httpStatus(event.error_status),
      errorKind = errorClass(event.error);
    if (this.retries.length < MAX_RETRIES) this.retries.push({ s: status, c: errorKind });
    const delay = Number(event.retry_delay_ms);
    this.retrySegs.push({
      a: now,
      d: Number.isFinite(delay) && delay > 0 ? Math.min(delay, 3_600_000) : 0,
      s: status,
      c: errorKind,
      i: this.inModel ? this.steps : this.steps + 1,
      q: ++this.seq,
    });
  }
  private noteModel(event: any) {
    if (
      event.type === "run_settings" &&
      typeof event.model === "string" &&
      event.model !== "default"
    )
      this.model ||= event.model;
    if (event.type === "system" && event.subtype === "init" && typeof event.model === "string")
      this.model = event.model;
  }
  private noteAssistant(event: any, now: number) {
    if (typeof event.message?.model === "string" && !this.model) this.model = event.message.model;
    for (const block of event.message?.content ?? []) {
      if (
        block?.type === "text" ||
        block?.type === "thinking" ||
        block?.type === "redacted_thinking"
      )
        this.output(now);
      else if (block?.type === "tool_use") {
        this.output(now);
        if (
          typeof block.id === "string" &&
          !this.tools.has(block.id) &&
          this.tools.size < MAX_TIMED_TOOLS
        )
          this.tools.set(block.id, {
            n: toolName(block.name),
            a: now,
            i: this.steps,
            q: ++this.seq,
          });
      }
    }
  }
  private noteToolResults(event: any, now: number) {
    for (const block of event.message?.content ?? []) {
      if (block?.type !== "tool_result") continue;
      const tool = this.tools.get(block.tool_use_id);
      if (tool && tool.b == null) {
        tool.b = now;
        if (block.is_error) tool.e = 1;
      }
      this.inModel = false; // the next output is a new model request
      this.lastResult = now;
    }
  }
  private noteQuestion(event: any, now: number) {
    if (event.type === "question" && typeof event.id === "string")
      this.waits.set(event.id, { a: now, q: ++this.seq });
    else if (event.type === "question_answer") {
      const wait = this.waits.get(event.id);
      if (wait && wait.b == null) wait.b = now;
    }
  }
  // Retry and rate-limit notices carry no output; true when the event was one of them.
  private noteNotice(event: any, now: number) {
    if (event.type === "system" && event.subtype === "api_retry") {
      this.noteRetry(event, now);
      return true;
    }
    if (event.type !== "rate_limit_event") return false;
    const status = event.rate_limit_info?.status;
    if (typeof status === "string" && status !== "allowed") this.rateLimits++;
    return true;
  }
  event(event: any, now = Date.now()) {
    if (!event || typeof event !== "object") return;
    if (fromProcess(event)) this.firstEvent ??= now;
    if (this.noteNotice(event, now)) return;
    this.noteModel(event);
    if (event.parent_tool_use_id) return;
    if (event.type === "stream_event" && event.event?.type === "content_block_delta")
      this.output(now);
    else if (event.type === "assistant") this.noteAssistant(event, now);
    else if (event.type === "user") this.noteToolResults(event, now);
    else this.noteQuestion(event, now);
  }
  finish(ev: any, now = Date.now(), spawnMs?: number | null): TurnTiming {
    const start = this.o.start,
      total = Math.max(0, now - start);
    const tools = [...this.tools.values()],
      waits = [...this.waits.values()];
    const wait = covered(waits, now);
    const tool = Math.max(0, covered([...tools, ...waits], now) - wait); // a tool waiting on the user counts as waiting
    const startup = Math.min(total, Math.max(0, (this.firstEvent ?? now) - start));
    const outcome =
      ev?.subtype === "aborted"
        ? "aborted"
        : ev?.subtype === "exited"
          ? "exited"
          : ev?.is_error
            ? "error"
            : "ok";
    return {
      turn_ts: start,
      provider: this.o.provider,
      model: this.model || this.o.alias,
      alias: this.o.alias,
      cold: this.o.cold ? 1 : 0,
      spawn_ms: this.o.cold && spawnMs != null && spawnMs >= 0 ? spawnMs : null,
      queue_ms: this.o.queueMs ?? null,
      first_ms: this.firstOutput != null ? this.firstOutput - start : null,
      total_ms: total,
      model_ms: Math.max(0, total - tool - wait - startup),
      tool_ms: tool,
      startup_ms: startup,
      wait_ms: wait,
      steps: this.steps,
      tools: tools.slice(0, MAX_TOOLS).map((t) => ({
        n: t.n,
        ms: Math.max(0, (t.b ?? now) - t.a),
        ...(t.e ? { e: 1 as const } : {}),
      })),
      retries: this.retryCount,
      retry_detail: this.retries,
      rate_limits: this.rateLimits,
      outcome,
      timeline: this.timeline(now, startup),
    };
  }
  // Ordered segments, offsets from the turn start. Metadata only, like the rest of the row.
  private timeline(now: number, startup: number): Timeline {
    const start = this.o.start,
      off = (t: number) => Math.max(0, t - start),
      dur = (a: number, b: number) => Math.max(0, b - a);
    const seg: Segment[] = [],
      order = new Map<Segment, number>();
    const add = (x: Segment, q: number) => {
      seg.push(x);
      order.set(x, q);
    };
    if (this.o.queueMs) add({ k: "queue", a: -this.o.queueMs, d: this.o.queueMs }, -1);
    if (startup > 0) add({ k: "startup", a: 0, d: startup }, 0);
    this.stepSpans.forEach((s, n) => {
      const last = n === this.stepSpans.length - 1;
      // A step ends at its last output; the final step runs on to the result unless it left a tool open.
      const end =
        last && ![...this.tools.values()].some((t) => t.i === n + 1 && t.b == null)
          ? now
          : (s.last ?? s.a);
      add(
        {
          k: "model",
          i: n + 1,
          a: off(s.a),
          d: dur(s.a, end),
          f: s.first != null ? dur(s.a, s.first) : null,
        },
        s.q,
      );
    });
    for (const t of this.tools.values())
      add(
        {
          k: "tool",
          i: t.i,
          n: t.n,
          a: off(t.a),
          d: dur(t.a, t.b ?? now),
          ...(t.e ? { e: 1 as const } : {}),
        },
        t.q,
      );
    for (const r of this.retrySegs)
      add({ k: "retry", i: r.i, a: off(r.a), d: r.d, s: r.s, c: r.c }, r.q);
    for (const w of this.waits.values())
      add({ k: "wait", a: off(w.a), d: dur(w.a, w.b ?? now) }, w.q);
    seg.sort((x, y) => x.a - y.a || order.get(x)! - order.get(y)!);
    return capTimeline(seg);
  }
}
export function capTimeline(seg: Segment[], max = MAX_SEGMENTS): Timeline {
  if (seg.length <= max) return { seg };
  const rest = seg.slice(max),
    more = {
      n: rest.length,
      from: rest[0].a,
      model_ms: 0,
      tool_ms: 0,
      other_ms: 0,
      tools: 0,
      errors: 0,
    };
  for (const s of rest) {
    if (s.k === "model") more.model_ms += s.d;
    else if (s.k === "tool") {
      more.tool_ms += s.d;
      more.tools++;
      if (s.e) more.errors++;
    } else more.other_ms += s.d;
  }
  return { seg: seg.slice(0, max), more };
}

// What the Run details panel shows for the last run; small, it rides on every thread update.
export function runFacts(t: TurnTiming) {
  const slow = [...t.tools].sort((a, b) => b.ms - a.ms).slice(0, 3);
  const slowest = slow[0];
  return {
    first_ms: t.first_ms,
    total_ms: t.total_ms,
    model_ms: t.model_ms,
    tool_ms: t.tool_ms,
    other_ms: t.startup_ms + t.wait_ms,
    steps: t.steps,
    tool_calls: t.tools.length,
    cold: !!t.cold,
    spawn_ms: t.spawn_ms,
    retries: t.retries + t.rate_limits,
    ...(slowest
      ? {
          slowest: { name: slowest.n, ms: slowest.ms },
          slow: slow.map((x) => ({ name: x.n, ms: x.ms, ...(x.e ? { error: true } : {}) })),
        }
      : {}),
    model: t.model,
  };
}

// Nearest-rank, like the Run details speed table: the p% sample of sorted times.
export const nearestRank = (sorted: number[], p: number) =>
  sorted.length ? sorted[Math.max(0, Math.ceil((p / 100) * sorted.length) - 1)] : null;
const pcts = (values: number[]) => {
  const s = values.filter(Number.isFinite).sort((a, b) => a - b);
  return { count: s.length, p50_ms: nearestRank(s, 50), p95_ms: nearestRank(s, 95) };
};
const round = (n: number | null) => (n == null ? null : Math.round(n));

const sumField = (rows: any[], key: string) =>
  Math.round(rows.reduce((total, row) => total + (row[key] || 0), 0));
const rowsOf = (rows: any[], provider: string) => rows.filter((row) => row.provider === provider);
function ttftRows(rows: any[], providers: string[]) {
  return [
    ...providers.map((provider) => ({
      provider,
      ...pcts(rowsOf(rows, provider).map((row) => row.first_ms)),
    })),
    ...(providers.length > 1
      ? [{ provider: "all", ...pcts(rows.map((row) => row.first_ms)) }]
      : []),
  ];
}
function phoneTtftRows(phone: { provider: string; total_ms: number }[]) {
  return [...new Set(phone.map((sample) => sample.provider))].sort().map((provider) => ({
    provider,
    ...pcts(
      phone.filter((sample) => sample.provider === provider).map((sample) => sample.total_ms),
    ),
  }));
}
function splitRows(rows: any[], providers: string[]) {
  return providers.map((provider) => {
    const own = rowsOf(rows, provider);
    return {
      provider,
      turns: own.length,
      model_ms: sumField(own, "model_ms"),
      tool_ms: sumField(own, "tool_ms"),
      other_ms: sumField(own, "startup_ms") + sumField(own, "wait_ms"),
      total_ms: sumField(own, "total_ms"),
    };
  });
}
function topToolRows(rows: any[]) {
  const tools = new Map<string, { ms: number[]; errors: number }>();
  for (const row of rows)
    for (const tool of row.tools) {
      const entry = tools.get(tool.n) || { ms: [], errors: 0 };
      entry.ms.push(tool.ms);
      if (tool.e) entry.errors++;
      tools.set(tool.n, entry);
    }
  return [...tools]
    .map(([name, entry]) => ({
      name,
      count: entry.ms.length,
      total_ms: Math.round(entry.ms.reduce((left, right) => left + right, 0)),
      ...pcts(entry.ms),
      errors: entry.errors,
    }))
    .sort((left, right) => right.total_ms - left.total_ms)
    .slice(0, 5);
}
function retryHistogram(rows: any[]) {
  const classes: Record<string, number> = {},
    statuses: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const row of rows)
    for (const detail of row.retry_detail) {
      classes[detail.c] = (classes[detail.c] || 0) + 1;
      if (detail.s != null) statuses[detail.s] = (statuses[detail.s] || 0) + 1;
    }
  return { classes, statuses };
}
function modelCounts(rows: any[]) {
  const models: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const row of rows)
    models[`${row.provider}/${row.model}`] = (models[`${row.provider}/${row.model}`] || 0) + 1;
  return models;
}
function recentRows(rows: any[]) {
  return rows
    .slice(-20)
    .reverse()
    .map((row) => ({
      turn_ts: row.turn_ts,
      thread: row.thread,
      provider: row.provider,
      model: row.model,
      cold: row.cold,
      first_ms: round(row.first_ms),
      total_ms: round(row.total_ms),
      model_ms: round(row.model_ms),
      tool_ms: round(row.tool_ms),
      steps: row.steps,
      retries: row.retries,
      outcome: row.outcome,
    }));
}

export class TurnTimingStore {
  private db: DatabaseSync;
  private writes = 0;
  private maxRows: number;
  private days: number;
  constructor(file: string, maxRows = 20_000, days = RETENTION_DAYS) {
    this.maxRows = maxRows;
    this.days = days;
    fs.closeSync(fs.openSync(file, "a", 0o600));
    fs.chmodSync(file, 0o600);
    this.db = new DatabaseSync(file);
    this.db.exec(`PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL; PRAGMA busy_timeout=2000;
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS turn_timing (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, at INTEGER NOT NULL, turn_ts INTEGER NOT NULL, thread TEXT NOT NULL, provider TEXT NOT NULL, model TEXT, alias TEXT,
        cold INTEGER NOT NULL, spawn_ms REAL, queue_ms REAL, first_ms REAL, total_ms REAL NOT NULL, model_ms REAL, tool_ms REAL, startup_ms REAL,
        wait_ms REAL, steps INTEGER, tools TEXT, retries INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, retry_detail TEXT, rate_limits INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, outcome TEXT NOT NULL
        , timeline TEXT
      ); CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS turn_timing_ts ON turn_timing(turn_ts); CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS turn_timing_thread ON turn_timing(thread, turn_ts);
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS agent_process (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, at INTEGER NOT NULL, thread TEXT NOT NULL, provider TEXT NOT NULL, kind TEXT NOT NULL, ms REAL, code INTEGER, signal TEXT, reason TEXT
      ); CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS agent_process_at ON agent_process(at);`);
    this.migrate();
    this.prune();
  }
  // Databases from before the timeline column gain it in place; their old rows simply have no timeline.
  private migrate() {
    const cols = (this.db.prepare("PRAGMA table_info(turn_timing)").all() as any[]).map(
      (c) => c.name,
    );
    if (!cols.includes("timeline"))
      this.db.exec("ALTER TABLE turn_timing ADD COLUMN timeline TEXT");
  }
  recordTurn(thread: string, t: TurnTiming, now = Date.now()) {
    const timeline = t.timeline
      ? JSON.stringify(
          t.timeline.seg.length > MAX_SEGMENTS ? capTimeline(t.timeline.seg) : t.timeline,
        )
      : null;
    this.db
      .prepare(`INSERT INTO turn_timing(at,turn_ts,thread,provider,model,alias,cold,spawn_ms,queue_ms,first_ms,total_ms,model_ms,tool_ms,startup_ms,wait_ms,steps,tools,retries,retry_detail,rate_limits,outcome,timeline)
      VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`)
      .run(
        now,
        t.turn_ts,
        thread,
        t.provider,
        t.model.slice(0, 80),
        t.alias.slice(0, 80),
        t.cold,
        t.spawn_ms,
        t.queue_ms,
        t.first_ms,
        t.total_ms,
        t.model_ms,
        t.tool_ms,
        t.startup_ms,
        t.wait_ms,
        t.steps,
        JSON.stringify(t.tools.slice(0, MAX_TOOLS)),
        t.retries,
        JSON.stringify(t.retry_detail.slice(0, MAX_RETRIES)),
        t.rate_limits,
        t.outcome,
        timeline,
      );
    this.wrote(now);
  }
  // kind: spawn | ready (ms = spawn -> first process output) | exit (ms = process lifetime; reason idle/settings/stop/compact/remove/shutdown/crash)
  recordProcess(
    p: {
      thread: string;
      provider: string;
      kind: "spawn" | "ready" | "exit";
      ms?: number | null;
      code?: number | null;
      signal?: string | null;
      reason?: string;
    },
    now = Date.now(),
  ) {
    this.db
      .prepare(
        "INSERT INTO agent_process(at,thread,provider,kind,ms,code,signal,reason) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
      )
      .run(
        now,
        p.thread,
        p.provider,
        p.kind,
        p.ms ?? null,
        Number.isInteger(p.code) ? p.code! : null,
        p.signal ? String(p.signal).slice(0, 16) : null,
        p.reason ? p.reason.slice(0, 16) : null,
      );
    this.wrote(now);
  }
  private wrote(now: number) {
    if (++this.writes >= 100) {
      this.writes = 0;
      this.prune(now);
    }
  }
  private prune(now = Date.now()) {
    for (const [table, col] of [
      ["turn_timing", "turn_ts"],
      ["agent_process", "at"],
    ]) {
      this.db.prepare(`DELETE FROM ${table} WHERE ${col} < ?`).run(now - this.days * 86400_000);
      this.db
        .prepare(
          `DELETE FROM ${table} WHERE id <= (SELECT id FROM ${table} ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1 OFFSET ?)`,
        )
        .run(this.maxRows);
    }
  }
  private static COLS =
    "id,at,turn_ts,thread,provider,model,alias,cold,spawn_ms,queue_ms,first_ms,total_ms,model_ms,tool_ms,startup_ms,wait_ms,steps,tools,retries,retry_detail,rate_limits,outcome";
  turns(from: number, to: number, thread?: string): any[] {
    const cols = TurnTimingStore.COLS; // aggregates never need the timelines
    const rows = (
      thread
        ? this.db
            .prepare(
              `SELECT ${cols} FROM turn_timing WHERE turn_ts BETWEEN ? AND ? AND thread = ? ORDER BY turn_ts`,
            )
            .all(from, to, thread)
        : this.db
            .prepare(
              `SELECT ${cols} FROM turn_timing WHERE turn_ts BETWEEN ? AND ? ORDER BY turn_ts`,
            )
            .all(from, to)
    ) as any[];
    return rows.map((r) => ({
      ...r,
      tools: JSON.parse(r.tools || "[]"),
      retry_detail: JSON.parse(r.retry_detail || "[]"),
    }));
  }
  // The thread's last `limit` turns, newest first, each with its timeline (null for rows recorded before timelines existed).
  recent(thread: string, limit = 10): any[] {
    const rows = this.db
      .prepare(
        `SELECT ${TurnTimingStore.COLS},timeline FROM turn_timing WHERE thread = ? ORDER BY turn_ts DESC, id DESC LIMIT ?`,
      )
      .all(thread, Math.max(1, Math.min(20, limit))) as any[];
    const parse = (s: any, d: any) => {
      try {
        return s ? JSON.parse(s) : d;
      } catch {
        return d;
      }
    };
    return rows.map(({ id, at, thread: _t, ...r }) => ({
      ...r,
      tools: parse(r.tools, []),
      retry_detail: parse(r.retry_detail, []),
      timeline: parse(r.timeline, null),
    }));
  }
  private exitCounts(from: number, to: number, thread?: string) {
    const exits: Record<string, number> = {};
    const procs = (
      thread
        ? this.db
            .prepare(
              "SELECT reason, COUNT(*) n FROM agent_process WHERE kind = 'exit' AND at BETWEEN ? AND ? AND thread = ? GROUP BY reason",
            )
            .all(from, to, thread)
        : this.db
            .prepare(
              "SELECT reason, COUNT(*) n FROM agent_process WHERE kind = 'exit' AND at BETWEEN ? AND ? GROUP BY reason",
            )
            .all(from, to)
    ) as any[];
    for (const proc of procs) exits[proc.reason || "unknown"] = proc.n;
    return exits;
  }
  // `phone`: browser-measured send -> first token painted samples (latency kind first_token), same window.
  summary(
    from: number,
    to: number,
    opts: { thread?: string; phone?: { provider: string; total_ms: number }[] } = {},
  ) {
    const rows = this.turns(from, to, opts.thread);
    const providers = [...new Set(rows.map((row) => row.provider))].sort();
    const cold = rows.filter((row) => row.cold);
    const retryKinds = retryHistogram(rows);
    return {
      from,
      to,
      retention_days: this.days,
      turns: rows.length,
      ttft: ttftRows(rows, providers),
      phone_ttft: phoneTtftRows(opts.phone || []),
      split: splitRows(rows, providers),
      tools: topToolRows(rows),
      cold: {
        turns: rows.length,
        cold: cold.length,
// ...
        spawn: pcts(cold.map((row) => row.spawn_ms).filter((value) => value != null)),
      },
      retries: {
        retries: rows.reduce((total, row) => total + row.retries, 0),
        rate_limits: rows.reduce((total, row) => total + row.rate_limits, 0),
        turns_affected: rows.filter((row) => row.retries || row.rate_limits).length,
        by_class: retryKinds.classes,
        by_status: retryKinds.statuses,
      },
      outcomes: rows.reduce(
        (counts: Record<string, number>, row) => (
          (counts[row.outcome] = (counts[row.outcome] || 0) + 1), counts
        ),
        {},
      ),
      exits: this.exitCounts(from, to, opts.thread),
      models: modelCounts(rows),
      steps: pcts(rows.map((row) => row.steps)),
      recent: recentRows(rows),
    };
  }
  close() {
    this.db.close();
  }
}
